===== V8 SIGNATURE SMOOTHNESS AND CINEMATOGRAPHY — COMPLETE NEW P0 CONTRACT =====


# V8 — THE CALM CINEMATIC MOTION SYSTEM | P0 PRODUCT CONTRACT

## The feeling (not just animation quantity)
The visitor must feel as if moving through **one continuous, tranquil architectural film**: soft, precisely directed, elegant, weighty, natural and relaxing. Motion serves wayfinding, spatial continuity and delight; it must never feel like a PowerPoint, jittery game camera, pop-up circus, flashy advertisement, or artificially slow app. It must remain immediately responsive to user input. Beauty without comfort, clarity, input responsiveness or stable frame times is FAILURE.

This document takes precedence over V1–V7 whenever motion requirements conflict. Preserve the four global sections Explore/Tour/Design/Amenities, 12 floors, 18 concept units, four Tour camera modes, four locales (AR default, EN, Sorani, ZH-CN), SOUND OFF, source-grounded Nakhla AI, all seven V7 engineering gates, safe optional Higgsfield for *offline marketing assets only*, and no unauthorized spend or deployment. **Do not add a fifth tab or a big new settings page.** A tiny contextual Motion Comfort preference is sufficient.

## Motion director / one ownership model (mandatory)
- Implement a central, typed **MotionDirector** or equivalent architecture for temporal coordination across the application. Its responsibilities: shared clock, camera pose/target/FOV, scene visibility and cutaway, UI affordances, materials, environmental transition, input lock, prefetch and cancellation/retargeting. React/router, GSAP/Motion (if chosen), Three.js and CSS must not fight over the same property.
- Camera transform has **one writer at any moment**. While a directed camera trip is running, orbit/free-look controls are temporarily blended/paused safely; on user interaction either cancel/retarget from the CURRENT rendered pose and velocity, or smoothly hand off to live controls. Never snap back to the original origin at cancellation.
- Use a monotonic clock and elapsed `dt` from the render frame, with time-based (frame-rate-independent) easing/damping. For frame-independent exponential decay use `alpha=1-exp(-lambda * dtSeconds)`; apply proper quaternion shortest-path spherical interpolation to orientations; never blindly lerp Euler angles across ±180°. Use bounded acceleration/jerk or a tuned critically damped spring for handoffs; don't stack smoothing twice or overshoot.
- Maintain explicit transition states: IDLE, PREPARING, PLAYING, PAUSED, CANCELLING, RECOVERING, REDUCED_MOTION. Typed transitions and one pending request policy: deduplicate double taps; last-intent-wins when safe; protect important user-confirmed state. Cleanly abort old prefetch and animation handles. Cancel/restart does not leak objects/events/tweens/RAF loops. Never gate user input on an arbitrary fixed timeout.
- Use real scene-space anchors (doorways, center of room, unit framing positions). No passing camera through walls, ceilings, window panes or furniture. Resolve physical routes via short spline/polyline through verified walkable navigation graph, with collision checks for camera and look target. If safe spatial path not possible, a graceful semantic cut/fade is preferable to clipping.
- Use at most one orchestration system for the SAME element; do not install a zoo of animation libraries. Prefer native CSS for simple composited UI, one tested timeline engine for choreographed multi-element UI, and render-loop delta-time for 3D camera. Bundle impact and licensing must be checked.

## The complete visual grammar — premium, peaceful, never sluggish
1. **Start / landing → Explore:** page is legible immediately; optional 2–3s tasteful title reveal may be skipped with one tap; 3D appears when ready without hard full-page white/black flicker. No forced intro on returning visitor unless deliberately requested.
2. **Building rotation:** low gain and gentle inertia; dragging begins visibly without noticeable dead zone, decelerates consistently, easy to stop; no orbit tilting upside down or camera crossing balconies, horizon remains comfortable. Auto-rotation OFF by default except deliberate cinematic guided tour.
3. **Tower → floor:** smoothly rotate to the chosen face if needed, visually isolate/lift target floor a modest amount, ease the camera to an intelligible medium shot, and keep selected-floor highlight stable. Motion direction must communicate where the floor came from; no random dissolve that hides spatial relationship.
4. **Floor → unit:** maintain framing continuity; ease the floor cutaway into an understandable dollhouse while moving camera toward selected unit. Preserve apartment and floor IDs exactly.
5. **Unit → room:** a deliberately paced, collision-safe dolly or smooth move with natural look-at lead, optionally a subtle short masked blend only when needed to avoid geometry occlusion. Arrive stable, no micro-oscillation or camera shake.
6. **Room → room:** use doorway/walkable path for nearby rooms, directional continuity and small arc-length-dependent duration; long travel should offer shortcut/skip, no exhausting forced flythrough.
7. **Switch 4 Tour camera modes:** align camera target, pose, FOV and velocities; stop old listeners and enter new control system on the same perceived scene. Never teleport between unrelated angles without a declared crossfade. Back and browser history preserve state.
8. **Amenities → back:** for long-distance jumps (apartment→F10 pool→F11 café), use graceful short transition with spatial anchor or discreet fade/cut, not absurd through-building camera travel.
9. **Material/color/finish:** prewarm/prefetch PBR textures and shaders; update canonical selected material atomically; allow a subtle 160–320ms reveal/crossfade if actually efficient. Avoid duplicate meshes/shaders and avoid a color that briefly disagrees with AI/property selection. On low quality, instant accurate material update beats expensive crossfade.
10. **Day→night/seasons/weather:** physically plausible smooth blend for exposure, sky/ambient, sun and shadows; weather visual state may be phased-in, not a heavy instant particle avalanche. Honor concept-vs-real daylight disclaimer.
11. **UI overlays / dropdown / AI sheet:** calm 180–320ms scale+opacity/translate with low visual travel, carefully timed text readability, natural dismissal, no layout shifts, no stacked blur-heavy backdrops. Mobile bottom sheets follow gesture without lag and settle naturally; preserve focus and escape/back action. Stagger only 20–40ms where useful, never 10 repeated cascades.
12. **Cinematic Day in Your Future Home / Presentation:** gently composed shot timing with continuity, limited meaningful camera journeys, natural pause, manual Skip/Exit, immediate control return. If visual smoothness drops, simplify effects before dropping user controls or facts.

## Timing & interpolation — tune, don't hard-code indiscriminately
The following are *initial tuning bands to validate with real users*, not fixed durations for all distances:
- feedback on tap/hover/focus: 60–140 ms perceived acknowledgment (the action must not wait for full transition)
- icon/button microinteraction: 110–180 ms
- tooltip/sheet/modal: 180–320 ms, with attention to mobile keyboard/focus
- section transition: ~360–650 ms for nearby narrative states; keep a way to skip/interrupt
- floor/unit camera framing: ~650–1100 ms, distance and device dependent
- nearby room guided move: ~650–1400 ms, based on verified path distance and rotation
- remote facility jump: ~300–600 ms with semantic bridge, not 3-second flight
- material reveal: ~160–320 ms; fallback immediate when expensive
- environment lighting/sky blend: ~600–1200 ms when not physically misleading
- intro/tour cinematic segments may be longer ONLY when opt-in and skippable.
Prefer critically damped/smoothstep-like or intentional cubic/quintic ease-in-out for composed moves; never apply abrupt linear speed or uniform movement to every object. Avoid excessive overshoot, bounce, wobble, elastic motion, continuous floating, parallax and fake handheld shake. Follow actual user intent: fast click gets immediate acknowledgment, transition feels gentle but is never an obstacle.

## Performance is part of the animation design — measured, not asserted
- 60Hz target on capable desktop corresponds to ~16.7ms/frame; 30Hz low-device viable fallback ~33.3ms/frame, not a promise of universal 60 FPS. For 120Hz device refresh, avoid artificially locking the whole app to 60 if measurement supports smoother rendering. Measure p50/p95/p99 frame time, missed-vsync/jank fraction, long JS tasks, FPS stability, GPU/CPU memory after 20+ scene swaps, page interaction latency and battery/thermal on physical phones. Do not fake screenshots/metrics.
- Proposed *conditional* release objective: capable desktop balanced profile: p95 frame duration <= 20ms while sampled transition is active with no repeated >50ms hitch; midrange mobile balanced/low: p95 <= 35ms and immediate Lite downgrade when sustained p95 worsens. Record frame-hitch count and profile conditions. These are project goals pending actual benchmarking and calibration, **not guarantees**. If devices fail, simplify real-time shaders, reduce dynamic resolution, shadow count, particles, textures and scene concurrency before impairing controls or substituting promotional video for live 3D.
- Ensure all textures/GLBs required by the destination are preloaded/decoded according to memory/network budget; show a stable preview/placeholder and never freeze the camera awaiting a network response. Compile shaders strategically; avoid first-frame stutter after selecting new material; chunk costly work off the input path and avoid blocking reflow. Respect storage and network data-saver settings. Reclaim graphics buffers and controls after changes.
- For HTML animations, default to `transform` and `opacity` instead of animated top/left/height/width or expensive `filter`/`backdrop-filter`. Use `will-change` *only for short-lived measured cases*, remove after the animation, and never place it globally on dozens of panels. Align scroll and 3D motion without multiple RAF schedulers.
- Demand real results on iOS Safari, Android Chrome, desktop Windows Chrome/Edge/Firefox and tablet Safari as available; emulators explicitly labeled; poor devices retain functional, visually calm Lite mode. A 60 FPS average with 400ms stalls is not smooth: track frame-time variance/hitches.
- Clamp the effect of unusually large frame delta after tab resume; do not fast-forward a camera trip into a wall or create huge spring velocities. Pause inactive guided animation and resume consistently; background mode should not consume GPU excessively.

## Comfort / accessibility / input discipline
- `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` is honored at first paint and when preference changes; reduce removes major spatial movement, automated camera fly-through, parallax and large perspective changes. Provide user-visible content and immediate non-spatial transitions; functionality and state remain intact.
- Offer a quiet contextual option **Motion: Gentle (default) / Reduced**; on OS reduced-motion default to Reduced. Option does not become a fifth top-level section. Never infer motion tolerance from disability or force a high-motion mode.
- A visible Skip/Stop/Pause/Back is available during *opt-in tours*; tapping/selecting something new interrupts correctly. Do not lock wheel, touch or keyboard indefinitely. Focus and screen-reader announcements follow stable destination semantics; no repeated announcements each animation frame. All four languages preserve layout and motion intent.
- On touch, a slight finger movement must not trigger unit selection while orbiting. Use drag/tap thresholds, pointer capture, pinch/rotation gesture arbitration and prevention of accidental double transitions. Keep tap-to-move and drag sensitivity adjustable by measured usability.
- Sounds and vibration OFF by default; no surprise music or mechanical whooshes; ambient audio only after user opt-in. All transitions must communicate without audio.

## Motion storyboard and engineering proof
Required deliverables by phase:
A. Pre-build: motion storyboard of 12 essential pathways with source/destination anchors, camera trajectory, duration envelope, focus intent, interruption semantics, lower-tier variant, reduced-motion variant, fallbacks.
B. First *working vertical slice*: exterior rotate -> select floor -> enter unit -> move living room -> swap material -> back to tower; full chain in actual browser with measured frame time and no glitches. Do this BEFORE broad site/content expansion.
C. Instrumentation: in-app DEV-ONLY motion HUD with FPS, frame-time sparkline, p95/p99 and input delay; sample per transition and device tier without leaking telemetry to public users. Export benchmark CSV/JSON and reproducible screen recordings.
D. Motion automated tests: test double-click/rapid retarget, immediate skip, cursor move while animation, low-memory, 4 locales, orientation, OS reduced motion change mid-transition, tab background/resume, WebGL context loss, offline, cold shader compile, repeated 20x navigation cycles, focus and historical Back. Include visual comparison at start, 25%, 50%, 75%, end; measure **motion continuity** beyond static golden screenshots.
E. Usability review: at least 3 representative first-time testers when available, including user watching both desktop and phone. Mark as unperformed if not actually recruited. Ask: 'did camera feel natural', 'could you interrupt instantly', 'was floor relation clear', 'was anything dizzying', 'did it feel responsive'. Collect evidence before declaring P0 pass.
F. Release decision: G8 motion smoothness gate is PASS only when representative real device data, interaction and reduced-motion checks satisfy acceptance and any failures are documented. V7 G1–G7 remain intact. Project remains NOT READY if any P0 is NOT_RUN/BLOCKED.

## Anti-patterns prohibited
Do NOT create artificial smoothness by adding a uniform 2-second ease on every button. No motion blur smearing text, fake slow motion, uninterruptible sequence, blanket crossfade between unrelated 3D states, 20 heavy postprocess passes, 10 layered CSS animations, camera bob, heavy mobile backdrop blur, exponential easing tied to frame count, unbounded long interpolation, camera overshoot into walls, transition-time memory leak, layout thrashing, random jitter, stalling until assets load, or pre-rendered Higgsfield videos presented as interactive 3D.

## Authoritative references and API checks
- Three.js OrbitControls: https://threejs.org/docs/pages/OrbitControls — enableDamping needs update() each frame; autoRotate frame independence needs delta time.
- GSAP responsive/reduced-motion helper: https://gsap.com/docs/v3/GSAP/gsap.matchMedia%28%29/ — a candidate if a coordinated timeline is actually necessary; evaluate bundle/license/duplication before installing.
- Chrome/web.dev performance animation guidance: https://web.dev/articles/animations-guide — prefer transform/opacity; profile paint/layout; will-change sparingly.
- Performance targets are planned user-defined quality thresholds and require measured validation, not product vendor guarantees.



# V8 GATE 8 — MOTION SMOOTHNESS, CINEMATIC COMFORT AND INTERRUPTIBILITY

This is a mandatory eighth release gate IN ADDITION TO V7 G1–G7. Do not rewrite/remove the original seven gates. A screenshot, prerecorded video, or marketing reel does NOT certify the interactive site. The intended feeling is exceptionally calm, luxurious and almost film-like without sluggish UI.

## Evidence required
1. Actual interactive vertical slice: building -> floor -> unit -> room -> material -> back; screen recording with clickable functionality plus frame-time profiling.
2. One camera/motion authority and lifecycle evidence: no competing tweens, no duplicate RAF/controls, no memory leaks after 20 cycles, stale input cancellation and retarget transitions from current pose.
3. Trace of 12 named motion flows and real mode-switching tests, with camera collision checks and visibly correct floor/unit spatial relationship.
4. Desktop and actual iOS/Android runs where accessible; report device/browser/version/resolution/GPU/quality tier/thermal condition/time/measurement tooling and med/p95/p99 frame times, hitches over 50ms. Emulator is NOT a physical device.
5. Reduced-motion desktop/mobile and OS preference changes during animation; keyboard, screen reader semantics, touchscreen gesture cancellation, contrast and four-locale layout stability.
6. Asset pipeline: predecode/warm needed materials or safe static preview, no blocking input, reduced-motion and Lite alternatives retained; developer-only motion HUD and replayable transition trace.
7. Human comfort evaluation, ideally >=3 first-time participants if available; if none, truthfully mark NOT_RUN and do not falsely claim user acceptance.

## Proposed thresholds — to be calibrated, not unconditional promises
- UI interaction acknowledgment perceptibly immediate: target <100ms for button response on supported hardware; expensive navigation can complete later with visible progress.
- Capable desktop on balanced quality: p95 active animation frame-time <=20ms, no repeated >50ms hitches across the defined vertical slice.
- Midrange phone balanced/low: p95 active frame-time <=35ms with adaptive reductions/fallback if exceeded.
- All critical transitions interrupt/cancel; no forced 2+ seconds waiting and no camera clipping.
- Every P0 test must show PASS evidence and a reproducible run. If benchmarks unavailable, label BLOCKED or NOT_RUN, not PASS.
- When unable to hit a proposed target on a device, prioritize a stable lower tier or Lite mode and document its limits. Avoid arbitrary frame-rate claims.

## Where to implement first
First implement V8 `MotionDirector` and the tower->floor->unit->room->material vertical slice; do not spend the first milestone building multiple cinematic intro videos. The visual direction comes from actual geometry and controlled motion, not external AI generation.

## QA files
- qa/V8_MOTION_QA_CASES.csv: 80 newly defined NOT_RUN cases; integrated into qa/QA_MASTER_MATRIX.csv.
- data/V8_MOTION_STORYBOARD.csv: 12 mandatory paths.
- data/v8_motion_contract.seed.json: motion vocabulary, accessibility policy, budget proposal.
- templates/V8_MOTION_BENCHMARK_TEMPLATE.csv: record real runs.

# V7 ENGINEERING QUALITY GATES — NEVER TRADE RELIABILITY FOR SPECTACLE

## Binding priorities and non-goals
The real product is a fast browser-based interaction, NOT a portfolio of unrelated AI images or videos. Keep four top-level sections Explore, Tour, Design, Amenities and a contextual Nakhla AI helper. The building has G + residential F01–F09 (two apartments each) + F10 wellness gym/spa/pool + F11 dining/cafe/separate shisha proposal. All dimensions are conceptual until explicitly validated. No regulations compliance or certification claims. The existing 550 unexecuted QA cases and all prior obligations remain.

### GATE 1 — Architectural source-of-truth and spatial traceability (P0)
- Define a canonical metric coordinate system, right-handed axes, consistent world origin, floor-to-floor levels, structural bays, core boundaries, doors/windows and georeference placeholder. Model architecture via authored parameter objects, CAD/BIM where possible, and deterministic Blender build scripts rather than ad hoc eye-balling.
- Export a floor-zone-room-surface graph. Every UI selector ID and Nakhla AI fact resolves to the same catalog item; each GLB mesh uses stable semantic IDs (not array indices). Generate 2D plans, 3D cutaways, dollhouse scene and room metadata from the same dimensions and transforms where feasible.
- Reconcile GFA, NFA and usable room areas with explicit conventions. Use polygon computations for floor areas, unit and room bounds, and signed-off geometry evidence. Distinguish approximate concept area from measured designed area; never assert as-built dimensions. No measured volume/area until geometry verification.
- Check unit-to-floor, room-to-unit, door adjacency, stairs/lift alignment across all 12 levels, stair landings, shaft stacking, collision hulls, balcony access, pool load sensitivity, restaurant vent/service routes, privacy and realistic elevations. Require qualified engineer review for actual construction.
- Produce geometry audit JSON/CSV mapping semantic ID => model node, plan polygon/area, transform, source, verified flag, validation timestamp. A user pointing to a ceramic floor should yield current catalog material, not an unrelated hallucinated sample.
- Automated assertions (not merely screenshots): building 12 levels and 18 units; child-to-parent consistency; no duplicate semantic IDs, no rooms outside apartment shell, no floor mismatch, collision coverage; all dimensional claims derived from audited authored geometry; error thresholds documented for renders-vs-metadata.

### GATE 2 — Physically plausible materials, architecture and lighting (P0)
- Use glTF 2.0 PBR workflow (roughness, metallic, normal, AO, realistic texel density) and Blender Principled BSDF with energy-consistent real-world scale. Set correct color management across Blender to WebGL; verify material tone in neutral 6500K and warm indoor scenes; avoid clipping whites, glossy concrete, impossible sharp reflections, tiled noisy normals, or self-emission masquerading as realistic lights.
- Use genuine plausible material dimension references: 60×120 porcelain displays correct 600×1200mm geometry and grout spacing; wood grain correct scale/direction, stone slab seams, glass physical transmission and shading; do not claim brand, durability or supplier unless validated.
- Verify shadows, contact/AO subtleties, reflection plausibility, correct handedness, railings, joints, thresholds, door hinges, floor interfaces, real-world heights and avatar-eye viewpoint. Match each 2D plan opening with a 3D opening.
- Physically based sun direction when actual site orientation and coordinates provided; if approximate, label as SIMULATED not actual proof of solar exposure. Seasonal rain, haze, dust shall be believable but degrade gracefully on mid/low devices.
- Render golden-master comparison images for at least exterior midday/dusk/night; Type A living/kitchen/bedroom/bath/balcony; F10 pool/gym; F11 restaurant/lounge; compare camera-matched 3D screenshot and Blender outputs. Document differences and approved target quality tier.
- Generated AI imagery is VISUALIZATION ONLY until matched to final architecture. Every gallery item records `derived_from_actual_scene`/`concept_ai_generated`, match scope and eligible use; never label as actual photograph or exact selected materials if not true.

### GATE 3 — Performance, predictable loading, smart streaming (P0)
- Load HTML/navigation/floor list FIRST; do not block on the hero 3D asset or autoplay video. Interactions available in Lite/2D while 3D warms up in a separate path. Use lazy imports and scene-based chunks, progressive GLB/asset downloads, AbortControllers, request prioritization, cancellation and correct disposal of scene resources.
- Compression experiments: profile before/after GLB + meshopt or Draco, GPU texture KTX2/BasisU, geometry quantization, texture atlas, instancing, draw-call reduction, LOD, occlusion/frustum culling, limited shadow casters, pre-baked lightmaps when appropriate. Preserve material ID mappings and collision graph across LOD levels. Compression is not assumed beneficial without measured decode/transfer tradeoffs.
- No importing all twelve floors and all interiors on first load. One live hero/exterior scene; next-scene preload only when memory/network budget allows; reclaim all old scene textures, geometry, event handlers and render loops on navigation to reduce peak memory and heat.
- Adaptive quality OFF/LITE/LOW/BALANCED/HIGH/ULTRA; derive from real feature support and observed FPS/frame time, battery preference, motion preference and memory stability, not UA substring alone. Cap DPR/pixel count; throttle or suspend rendering when idle/tab hidden; never constantly render idle 60fps purely for ambience. Allow manual override and preserve semantics.
- Candidate budgets, not guaranteed: on adequate mobile 4G, aim shell interactive with LCP <=2.5s, INP <=200ms, CLS <=0.1 (p75 field targets). 3D initial scene should progressively appear with visible loading status; set measured budgets after hardware profiling. Target sustained 45–60 FPS balanced on capable desktop and >=30 FPS on representative midrange phone in normal walking; if not achievable, degrade detail before usability. Include low-end 20–30 FPS fallback. These FPS are project goals, NOT pre-certified metrics. Collect frame time p50/p95 and long tasks; test thermal throttling (5+ min walk), app memory, data transferred, GPU context resets and stale cache.
- Compress photos AVIF/WebP + responsive sizes, semantic alt, CSS aspect ratios. Separate cinematic trailer download from core visitor assets; never auto-download HD videos on cellular. Budget video sizes and use reduced-motion static posters.
- Serve build optimized over CDN with cache/versioned immutable asset hashes; robust cache invalidation after release, no stale GLB/data mismatch. Offline/small-network fallback must never expose fictitious 'ready' indicators.

### GATE 4 — Human-friendly camera, tours and non-nauseating navigation (P0)
- Shared single camera authority with cancelable state transitions. Preserve all selections when switching Cinematic, Guided, Free Walk, Property Explorer. Undo/back/reset, clear position, breadcrumbs and map button; escape key and no pointer-lock traps.
- Real-world eye height around 1.55–1.7m unless accessibility/seat view requires change; hard turn/pitch limits, acceleration and deceleration, proportional sensitivity, wall/door/balcony collision, no teleport into geometry. Prevent roll and violent FOV spikes, keep zoom/pan sane; tap-to-move on mobile, optional gentle virtual stick only when needed.
- Navmesh/path planning aligned with actual floors/doors/obstacles and verified height differences; verify stairs/elevator transition is accessible and does not clip walls. Restrict access to undeveloped/locked zones with accurate messaging, not invisible ghost barriers. Accessibility and motion-safe settings, reduced motion overrides autoplay and camera sweeps.
- Avoid motion stacking, runaway camera tween, accidental floor changes while gestures, touch scrolling trapped by canvas, controls under the keyboard or unsafe mobile safe areas. Touch targets >=44 CSS pixels when possible, focus-visible/keyboard navigation.
- Tour mode is optional and stoppable; if the visitor presses UI control, interrupted transition yields control smoothly without resetting selected floor, material overrides, environment or chat context.

### GATE 5 — Robust recovery & graceful degradation (P0)
- Catch WebGL context lost/restored and handle it using resource restoration procedures; test using supported context loss simulation where possible. A permanently broken canvas cannot block selectors, contact details, AI factual answers or available floor plans.
- Per-scene error boundaries, timeout and retry-with-backoff; loading state meaningful; prevent infinite spinner. On network interruption preserve the selection in memory/url; retry only idempotent GET operations; no repeated sales submit.
- On repeated graphics/decoder failure offer 2D Interactive Plan or image-based Lite with accessible navigation and all project facts. Mark the demo fallback honestly; do not call an image a live editable PBR scene.
- Support tab suspend/resume, resize and screen orientation, mobile back/forward, BFCache if feasible, hot reload, slow OS memory. Dispose resources and subscriptions; test no cumulative growth after 20 switch cycles.
- Loss of Higgsfield access, promo expiry or any other vendor cannot take down site. All generated promotional assets must be pre-exported and legally usable or removed.

### GATE 6 — Objective visual regression and all-locale QA (P0)
- Use Playwright (and appropriate WebGL/browser setup) for golden screenshot baselines with deterministic camera, locale, timezone, environment preset and seed. Test Arabic RTL, Sorani RTL, English LTR, Simplified Chinese LTR, glyph shaping, font fallback, safe areas and horizontal overflow on phone/tablet/laptop/desktop.
- Baselines cover default hero, dropdown/panel on touch, 10th/11th floors, walk mode, 3 material switches, 4 weather/season variants, 2D fallback, popup/error, AI bottom sheet, PDF UI and dark/bright environment contrast. Make auto-diff thresholds configurable, compare text positioning independently of 3D antialias noise.
- End-to-end tests should interact, not solely assert DOM. Assert material selection actually updates a GPU surface, scene transition preserves user state, audio OFF after reload, environment state preserved, 4 locale semantic equality, typed AI actions and no invented facts.
- Accessibility automated audit with manual keyboard/screen-reader checks; matrix Safari iOS, Chrome Android, desktop Chrome/Edge/Firefox and tablet Safari. Record actual device coverage and avoid reporting emulators as real devices.
- First-load, warm-load, slow 3G/4G, offline recovery, WebGL disabled, context lost, tab switching, keyboard open, orientation change, reduced motion and text zoom. Every P0 failure must be fixed or a clearly documented P0 release blocker, never silently waived.

### GATE 7 — Privacy-first operational quality monitoring (launch gate)
- Collect aggregated performance telemetry only on an opt-in or lawful privacy basis after user has approved deployment: Core Web Vitals, long-task/frame-time distribution, failed asset class, scene unload/load, crash rate, device tier, request latency and anonymized error signatures. NO webcam, microphone, raw chat, precise GPS, full personal IP or user text logging by default.
- Avoid leaking contact inquiries/AI content via analytics providers. No external trackers by default without consent and privacy policy. Use opt-in quality metrics UI where legally required. Retention period/consent/revocation explicit; developer dashboard password protected; no secrets in browser and throttled logging.
- Monitor cache version mismatches, image compression decode failures, stale asset 404s, CDN error trends and context loss; define rollback plan with known-good bundle and original floor/material metadata.
- Publish only with owner approval and documented release manifest/QA evidence. Have an operational checklist for data backup, versioning, accessibility and real-user support channels.

## EXTRA V7 risk items that must be carried forward
- Pool on F10 is a structurally significant load and needs engineer validation of beam/slab loading, waterproofing, humidity, access, plant room and maintenance; do not assert feasibility from concept render.
- Shisha on F11 requires legally vetted plan, isolated terrace, fire and smoke separation; ventilation alone does not make smoke safe, never market an unapproved indoor shisha service as available.
- Real views from balcony only when source photography/map/model matches approved geolocation/height/direction; otherwise show clearly labelled 'conceptual view'.
- If owner has no real licensed CAD, create documented fictional example with explicit `CONCEPT • NOT BUILT` watermark on technical/detailed pages, and no real sales claims.
- Four navigation modes are internal to Tour; four global site sections EXACTLY as previous. Higgsfield is NOT a public user feature, section, required API or runtime dependency.

Gate H0 Higgsfield: can be PASS = verified licensed zero-cost supported flow with owner approval; SKIPPED = safely completed with Blender; NEVER BLOCKS WEBSITE SHIPMENT solely by its absence. Commercial rights must be checked for any media retained.

### Performance SLO proposal (NOT proven)
Core Web Vitals good thresholds p75: LCP<=2.5s, INP<=200ms, CLS<=0.1 for actual devices/users; measure real and label emulation. Target 3D balanced capable desktop >=45 FPS (aspire 60) and mid-tier phone >=30, after thermal test; adaptive fallback if missed. No automated test can prove guarantee for every device. Define shipping-grade performance from measurement and realistic support matrix, not aspirational feature list.

### Source truth, finance, licensing
Pool roof loads, indoor moisture, shisha fire/ventilation, local approvals are concepts only. No pricing/availability/brand/warranty/room dimension invented. Higgsfield trial defaults disabled; not subscribed without explicit owner action; no vendor-dependent visitor runtime; audit asset licenses.

YOU ARE THE EXECUTING LEAD, NOT AN ADVISER WRITING A PROMPT FOR SOMEBODY ELSE.
Act as an integrated principal architect, Basra-aware climate design researcher, senior real estate product designer, senior React/Three.js engineer, expert Blender 3D artist, PBR/material scientist for visualization, cinematic motion director, accessibility specialist, visual QA lead, cybersecurity/licensing reviewer and project delivery manager. Work in evidence-based phases, making professional reversible decisions without requiring the human to micromanage. Verify what you actually do. The output is a REAL, polished, running interactive WEBSITE and complete editable source project, NOT a single render, slideshow, Reels video, empty React template, mockup or plan-only document.

USER'S INTENT, EXACTLY:
A visiting client first sees a refined name/brand and stunning realistic 3D architecture, then moves around an original building. Each of the 12 levels is selectable and highlights on hover/tap; 01-09 are residential with two selectable units per level. The visitor can enter a selected floor and choose unit and room; camera flows to a credible 3D interior. Clickable floor/wall/wood/kitchen/bath surfaces display rich material specifications, and switching choices REALLY changes the 3D scene, not just metadata. User explores photorealistic stills/gallery afterward, can compare finishes, save a personalized finish board, and request a demo quote. On Level 10, they explore premium gym, pool and wellness; on Level 11 restaurant, café, separate conditional shisha terrace and panoramic lounge. The site's feel is the high-end Instagram 3D property reel supplied as *visual inspiration*, upgraded into actual usable accessible software. This is a portfolio pitch to future Basra real-estate companies, not marketing for a real extant building.

MAKE THESE FIXED CONSTRAINTS NON-NEGOTIABLE:
- Project: NAKHLA RESIDENCE / نخلة ريزيدنس, as an ORIGINAL FICTIONAL Basra-inspired Modern Iraqi Luxury tower. Brand can be changed only with documented strong reason, but keep Nakhla as default.
- Level arithmetic: G (ground) + 01–09 (nine residential floors) + 10 (wellness/gym/pool) + 11 (restaurant/café/terrace) = 12 OCCUPIED LEVELS IN TOTAL; NOT 12 residential + 2 amenities. Roof service zone is separate unoccupied equipment area.
- Nine residential floors x two homes (A and B) = 18 selectable units. Early concept unit Type A 125 m² approx/2 beds/2 baths; B 145 m² approx/3 beds/2 baths. Hero Type A five detailed areas (living, kitchen, master bed, bath, balcony); other room geometry remains coherent and truthful.
- Exterior: actually interactive 3D model with semantic floor picking. Interior: real interactive 3D for chosen hero room surfaces; use excellent Blender renders for photoreal showcase as needed. Hybrid is acceptable; **flat fake 3D screenshots cannot be passed off as genuine free camera navigation or live surface editing**.
- Materials: six core categories, three base choices each, inspect true sample metadata and swap actual PBR materials live. Compare, packages, undo/redo, reset, persistence and export selection. Never assert false brands/origin/price/certification; demo samples clearly labeled.
- Amenities: gym/pool/spa/recreation zone, restaurant/café and clearly separated shisha area conditional on feasibility and legal rules. No unsupported claims about elevated pool safety or smoke removal.
- Interface: premium/quiet cinematic, responsive, Arabic RTL and English LTR, mouse/touch/keyboard, accessibility, reduced motion and 2D fallback. Fully functioning controls, no fake inquiry success.
- Full project ownership inside NEW workspace, versions pinned and documented. Free/open licensed tools/assets by default. User wants you to research and install suitable skills, but never install malicious/unverified software, blindly execute remote scripts, spend money or disable system safeguards.
- Do as much work as possible without unnecessary human questions. You ARE authorized to decide tradeoffs, aesthetics, architecture concept and implementation details through deep research. You are NOT authorized to pay, deploy publicly, contact people, access personal secrets, modify nonproject files or obtain elevated/system privileges without consent.

Vetted reputable relevant skills only, check licenses and hidden scripts, pin versions, maintain reversible manifests. Consider Lemo-Opuscar only if producing a companion video. Do not pretend skills installed if unable.

If session time/tool capacity prevents completion, leave durable working project + exact RESUME_HERE.md, explain completion state, no claim it kept running after session/power loss.

8. When constraints conflict, prioritize: user safety/legality > honest functionality > original visual quality > architectural consistency > performance/accessibility > optional flourish. Document exceptions.

PROJECT SUCCESS TEST (THE FIVE-MINUTE DEMO):

All buttons work on desktop and mobile, with graceful non-WebGL fallback. The same geometry/labels appear in 2D plans, 3D exterior and room interiors. This must be genuinely demonstrated, not narrated as if it exists.

DOCUMENT: PROJECT_CHARTER.md
===========================================================================
# Project charter — Nakhla Residence | نخلة ريزيدنس

**Purpose:** A premium interactive, bilingual (Arabic/English), photorealistic-leaning online apartment and amenities exploration and finishes configurator. Commercial portfolio demonstration for possible Basra property developers; original fictional concept, not a real property listing.

**Brand direction:** Modern Iraqi Luxury: warm ivory/travertine-look limestone, muted bronze metal, rational sun shading, high-quality insulated glazing, real balconies, palms, refined signage, calm desert-river climate ambiance, modern typography. No imitation of an identifiable existing development.

**Model:** 12 occupied levels in the showcased concept: ground G; nine residential floors 01–09; floor 10 wellness/gym/indoor pool/sauna/changing/relaxation with proper separation; floor 11 rooftop restaurant/café and separately ventilated/open-air shisha lounge (conditional legal/regulatory feasibility). Not an engineering permission to build.

**Residential concept:** 18 apartments in total, two per typical residential floor, concept Type A approximately 125 m² with 2 bedrooms, Type B approximately 145 m² with 3 bedrooms; area definitions and exact dimensions require verification and consistent scheduling. One hero apartment developed in deep detail; another distinctly plausible plan at medium fidelity, all 18 units real selectable data-driven instances. Never present repeated layouts as 18 individually architect-designed distinct units.

**Primary experience:** Cinematic brand reveal -> 3D tower exterior -> per-floor hover/select -> exploded level -> unit selection -> accurately mapped dollhouse -> room enter and orbit/walk controls -> interactive material inspection and live swapping -> package comparison and finish board -> consistent photo gallery -> save/share/export -> demo inquiry/contact. Cross-level amenities accessible equally; cinematic guided tour is optional and interruptible.

**Design principle:** The facade, 2D plan, 3D plan, interior, camera anchors and material specifications must all describe the SAME geometry. Never let marketing imagery contradict unit details, material settings, floor positions, area counts or room locations.

**Audience:** Sales representatives, consumers, project developers, investors, architecture offices. Device classes: premium desktop, average laptop, modern phone, constrained phone/low power/reduced motion. All clickable UI controls must work; alternative 2D layouts must remain available when WebGL is unavailable.

**Commercial conversion:** Demonstrate inquiry, requested quote, selections summary, PDF export as genuine working local demo flow (not falsely claiming submissions reach real company). No real online payments, bookings or actual inventory integration without explicit approval.

**Non-goals:** Production construction documentation, structural/MEP sign-off, advertising an actual existing property, copying another business's brand/assets, guaranteed realtime ray-traced photorealism on any device, unsafe automatic npm/skill/OS execution.

**Authority:** Claude may research and decide responsible implementation details autonomously; explicit fixed constraints outrank aesthetic suggestions. A qualified local architecture/structures/MEP/fire/safety professional must review any real-world proposed building. Climate design recommendations are provisional until site orientation and analysis exist.

**Finish line:** Running website with working major flows, original assets or documented licensed assets, automated tests and visual review evidence, source files, README/setup script, asset provenance, limitations, measured performance, handoff preview/screenshots and a concise accurate delivery report.



===========================================================================
DOCUMENT: specs/ARCHITECTURE_BRIEF.md
===========================================================================
# Architectural concept and parametric brief

## One geometry, many outputs
One canonical metric-unit data model drives Blender geometry, floor plates, viewer interaction, plan labels, marketing areas, hotspot anchors, camera poses and renders. Establish coordinate system, origin and floor elevation grid. Never freehand a photograph independently from unit plan. Produce elevations/plans/sections as *concept visualization* with clear disclaimer, not for permitting or construction.

## Site and massing
Earlier planning assumption: hypothetical 24m x 32m parcel (~768m²), tentative tower footprint 18m x 24m (~432m²). These are DESIGN HYPOTHESES ONLY. A 3m side margin and other setbacks, coverage, parking, access, code, sunlight, area, lobby/plant rooms, stair sizing and evacuation route feasibility must be tested. The earlier numerical assumptions may be mutually incompatible with actual site/regulations; do not silently treat as lawful. Allow Claude to revise footprint/plot size and provide calculations. Do not invent a real street address or waterfront view. If using a notional streetscape, label it conceptual Basra atmosphere, not real drone imagery.

## Floors and numbering
G: lobby, secure entry, mail/parcel, reception, management/security, service rooms, shared circulation, separate amenity access, service/loading, possible parking approach (parking demand is not solved by one pad). Lobby may be double height but do not pretend an extra occupied floor exists; model double-height volumetric opening accurately.
01-09: two apartments per floor, opposite orientations where feasible, shared lobby, elevator(s) after capacity study, protected exit stairs as validated, vertical MEP/service shafts. Bounded sound/privacy adjacency. Accessible paths, balconies, daylight and ventilation with actual window placement.
10: wellness club with safe proposed gym, pool, wet areas, changing rooms, reception, equipment/service rooms and MEP; pool in an elevated location requires structural loading, waterproofing, leak detection, humidity/condensation treatment, slab deflection, vibration, emergency egress and acoustics feasibility. Provide an architectural alternative — move pool to low-level podium or roof terrace while preserving virtual highlight — if feasibility fails. Never present the level as signed off.
11: restaurant + specialty café + panoramic terrace + shisha zone only if allowed by local codes/policies and with separate/safe air paths; isolate smoke from apartments, fresh-air intakes and nonsmoking patrons. Kitchen grease extraction, service elevator/path, fire suppression, deliveries, drainage, food safety, grease traps, waste, acoustics, and elevator traffic must be addressed conceptually. Prefer exterior shisha area and clear separation; if feasibility impossible, alternative non-smoking terrace lounge.
Roof: controlled maintenance/service equipment; not an occupied Floor 12.

## Concept dimensions and consistency
Starting concept residential gross: 432m² plate; unit A 125m², B 145m² => 270m² combined; ~162m² plate available for commons, vertical shafts, structure and wall thickness depending on definition. This is not an exact fit-out calculation; confirm net vs gross, circulation, setbacks and balcony treatment. Express area uncertainty honestly, and avoid fabricated construction feasibility. Floor-to-floor elevations derived from one source; no floating levels or intersecting stairs.

## Exterior visual language
- Base/mid/crown hierarchy; dark tactile base cladding, warm-ivory stone midsection, subtle bronzed aluminum vertical fins and window frames, generous recesses and shaded usable balconies, elegant crown/roof screen.
- Keep facade elevations aligned to actual room apertures: living room/front balcony, bedrooms/quieter openings, kitchens/ventilation, stair/service strips. Window/grid variation must derive from plan, not random facade detailing.
- Environment: palms and restrained native-inspired landscaping, shaded pedestrian approach, compliant/drop-off/vehicle separation, no impossible vehicle circulation. Drainage/maintenance/weathering considered for Basra heat, UV, dust, salt/humidity variability.
- Materials with true PBR roughness/normal/displacement where suitable; real edges/chamfers, glass thickness/IOR where physically useful, measured tone mapping and controlled exposure. Avoid glossy plastic-looking stone, opaque black glass, repeated clone vegetation.
- Day/night controls: realistic changes to sky, sun, façade luminance and interior lights; avoid overexposed neon night scenes.

## Unit plans and room adjacency
Type A: foyer -> living/dining -> shaded balcony; semi-open kitchen and real ventilation; master + ensuite; secondary bedroom; shared bath; laundry/storage; access/circulation. Type B: 3-bedroom alternative with living/dining, separated kitchen optional, master+ensuite, shared bath, optional guest WC if feasible; no phantom rooms.
For every unit supply room polygons (metric), area, door swings, window openings, wall thickness, path graph, accessibility criteria as feasible, room IDs, material slot IDs, camera anchor, level ID, east/north assumptions. Furnish realistically with correct sizes/clearances; evaluate actual room usability, not just visual richness.

## Building services and codes
Before claiming compliance, research and cite current local municipal building rules and competent licensed professionals. Verify feasibility of two exit stairs and elevator capacity, fire egress, occupant load, wind and seismic considerations, pool load and isolation, restaurant MEP, shisha rules, electrical backup, refuse, water pressure, parking, accessible entry. Include a "requires licensed review" badge in technical deliverables. Optimize conceptual presentation, not permit approvals.

## Deliverable outputs
Original .blend authored assets, parametric Python scripts, facade elevations, floor stack, site diagram, floor plans, room polygons, glb exports with semantic clickable mesh IDs, still-render hero scenes (day/night) and scene-to-UI coordinate map. Keep measured dimensional consistency, scene naming conventions and visual QA comparisons across views.

DOCUMENT: specs/UX_EXPERIENCE_MAP.md

# UX and interactive journey specification

## Universal rules
Treat the experience as a stateful spatial product rather than 7 unrelated marketing pages. Route/state graph is explicit, URL-shareable where safe, keyboard and touch accessible, deep-linkable to project/floor/unit/room/material package. Transitions never erase selections. All interaction affordances must communicate actionable status. Provide skip animation, reduced motion, reset navigation, back, breadcrumbs, loading and recoverable errors.

## Screen 01: Cinematic entrance
- Intro logo/project name "NAKHLA RESIDENCE | نخلة ريزيدنس", Basra-inspired fiction disclosure. Reveal structure/massing progressively with 3D camera, sky and material lighting; restrained filmic typography, optional mute-controlled original ambient score.
- CTAs: Explore Tower, Take Guided Tour, Skip Intro, language toggle, Sound On/Off. Every button must perform expected state change. Desktop CTA and mobile CTA accessible without waiting for video.
- No fixed blocking 10s intro. Can enter instantly on click; respect reduced motion; first meaningful paint remains fast; poster fallback until GLB ready.

## Screen 02: Exterior Building Explorer
- Animated orbit and drag orbit, constrained pan/zoom, 10? NO: 12 occupied levels, G + 01–11; floor selectors all appear including F10 and F11 special-use.
- Hover floor: semi-transparent warm highlight via semantic mesh floor segment or accurate raycast; descriptive label; no z-fighting. Click floor: persistent selection, floor metadata summary, button Explore Floor. Selection via dropdown/keyboard and accessible list. Highlight changes on touch selection and keyboard focus.
- Camera bookmarks: front/side/back/aerial, Reset View, Day/Night, Building X-Ray, cinematic guide start, orientation compass/scale where useful. X-Ray must truthfully reveal modelled interiors; unmodelled rooms clearly represented with simplified accurate volumes, not fake furnished detailed rooms.
- Loading on demand; mobile fallback floor selector works even without WebGL.

## Screen 03: Floor/Unit Explorer
- Click selected residential floor -> gentle extraction or cutaway of actual floor slab. No visual interpenetration or exploding through the occupied floor; stable lighting/exposure. Display core (lift, stairs, corridor), A and B as selectable boundary meshes.
- Unit data: identifier 04A etc, concept floor area 125/145m², 2/3 beds, baths (from data), balcony, conceptual availability status and no fictional purchase prices without demo label. Show north arrow, room labels, floorplate clipping and accessible 2D fallback.
- Hover unit outlines selected apartment polygon; click transitions to isometric dollhouse with same walls, openings and doors. Restore camera if navigating back; breadcrumb Tower > Floor 04 > Unit 04A > Living Room.
- For F10 show amenity zone map with pool/gym/sauna/changing/wellness lounge. For F11 restaurant/cafe/shisha terrace (if conditionally supported); never show two apartments on amenity levels.

## Screen 04: Apartment Dollhouse and Interior
- Isometric cutaway roof removed; room contours semantic; click each room to interpolate to interior camera anchor with collision constraints and no teleport nausea. Real geometry (walls, windows, furniture and light), not image mistaken for full 3D.
- Rooms in initial hero Type A: living/dining, kitchen, master bedroom, bathroom and balcony; secondary bedroom and other rooms represented spatially and selectable with honest reduced-detail render if not polished.
- Modes: guided room hotspot navigation, orbit/inspect, 360 look, camera presets, optional free-walk only if performant and bounded; interactive furniture toggle if realistic; 2D plan mini-map. Avoid interiors facing blank walls or clipping through ceilings.
- Selection markers only on supported material slots; outline at object level with visible text description.

## Screen 05: Material Studio
- A selected surface brings contextual side panel, accessible labels and material information: brand/type/origin/size/finish/performance/spec sheet ONLY if verified; fictional placeholder otherwise "Concept / sample / manufacturer TBD".
- 6 categories in version one: flooring, wall paint, wood finish, kitchen cabinet, countertop, bathroom tile. Three options per category, at minimum 18 sample SKUs in seed. Not all categories applied to every room.
- Direct 3D changes affect corresponding object material/UV/roughness in all accessible views; avoid modifying the entire building by shared material reference accidentally. Persist design choices per unit+room/surface; package presets Standard/Premium/Luxury may be suggested, with clear fictional pricing/no authoritative material costs. Allow Undo/Redo, Reset to Base, compare A/B and Before/After consistent viewpoint, accessible save and share.
- Material selectors show true samples plus warnings on unsuitable material uses (e.g. wet-area slip resistance, outdoor weather), no speculative certified properties. Prevent prohibited combinations and explain why.

## Screen 06: Photorealistic Gallery
- Responsive slider, keyboard left/right (respect RTL semantics), thumbnails, fullscreen, touch swipe, alt text, zoom/pan carefully, no layout jump. All images legitimate self-made renders/licensed. Include exterior (day/night), double-height lobby, main living, kitchen, bedroom, bathroom, balcony, gym, pool and restaurant.
- Images correspond to pre-rendered finish packages; if user customized beyond preset, label "Representative rendering – selected live 3D materials may differ", or render genuine snapshot from current WebGL scene with accurate settings. Never silently show wrong materials as selected final image.

## Screen 07: Saved Apartment and Inquiry
- View selected floor/unit/rooms, materials by category+surface, IDs, display available verified spec metadata, chosen aesthetic package, daytime image, safe amount placeholders as labeled.
- Save locally with a clear notification; privacy-first with opt-in storage, no secrets, robust reset and share URL size controls. Generate client-side PDF or printable HTML using user-selected details with fiction/disclaimer and original imagery. Contact/quote form must validate name/contact/country code and consent before submission; without backend, present "Demo only: request saved locally, not sent". Never display success saying company was contacted when no integration exists.
- Conversion calls: Request Quote, Schedule Viewing (demo), Download Finish Board, Share Unit, Contact Sales (demo channel disclaimer). No payment flow.

## Guided presentation mode
- 90–150 second optional narrative without an assumed literal duration: brand -> wide tower -> floor highlight -> cutaway -> hero unit -> living room -> live material swap -> wellness -> restaurant -> saved selection. User can pause, skip, jump, mute. All animations deterministic and capture-friendly for sales demos.
- Provide autoplay only after explicit Start Tour, never hijack scrolling. Each shot has reason, camera path, composition, text and fallback.

## Cross-cutting interaction matrix
- Mouse, touch, keyboard, screenreader-supported alternative list. Visible focus styles, contrast, aria-live only where necessary.
- Localization: Iraqi-friendly Modern Standard Arabic copy; locale-aware number formatting, correct RTL layout and arrows, English full translation. Do not embed Arabic text into textures; HTML text overlays remain accessible and responsive.
- Responsive: desktop side-panel, tablet sheets, mobile bottom drawer; touch target ≥44px as target, no hover-only access. Support portrait/landscape orientation.
- Loading: skeleton with poster / progress meter for external GLB/texture load; progressive reveal; retry; timeout; 2D fallback if WebGL unavailable. No fake progress percentages.
- Errors: missing asset/failed GPU shader/connection loss/back navigation/broken deep link/reduced motion. Every mode can escape to a functioning page.

DOCUMENT: specs/3D_PRODUCTION_PIPELINE.md
===========================================================================
# 3D asset production, photorealism and website export

## Ground rules and asset traceability
All visuals must be legally usable in a commercial portfolio. Reference reel frames are for *style/interaction analysis only*. Never embed an actual copyrighted facade, reference video frame, company logo or unique floor plan as production art without permission. Favor an ORIGINAL Blender exterior and interior; CC0/clearly commercially licensed furniture/materials are allowed if source/license retained. Free does NOT necessarily mean commercial use permitted. Attribute every third-party library, model, HDRI, texture, icon, sound and font.

## Production stages
P0 Preflight: detect GPU/VRAM/CPU/RAM/disk and installed Blender version, browser/WebGL availability, version controlled repo, render engine supported. User context suggests PC Ryzen 5 3600 and RTX 3050, but probe actual machine; never assume GPU memory quantity. Select Eevee for iterations; Cycles for selective hero renders if feasible, use samples/noise controls appropriate to available hardware. If Blender absent and installation is safe, select official installation workflow for Windows; installation requires responsible permission when modifying system outside project.
P1 Single-source architecture: generate parametric building shell from floor grid, facade modules, elevators/stairs/service volumes, level IDs. Explicit naming: BLDG_L02_FLOOR_PICK, UNIT_L04_A_ZONE, SURFACE_UNIT_A_LIVING_FLOORING, etc. Do not create hundreds of ambiguous "Cube.001" objects. Validate number of levels and height invariants with script.
P2 Signature exterior: detailing at 3 scales: silhouette, medium-scale bands/shades/recessed balconies, micro-details at camera proximity: realistic window mullions, seams, handles, slab edges, drips, landscaping and architectural lighting. Even large forms must avoid toy-like proportions.
P3 One accurate typical residential floor: create occupancy zones, core and two coherent unit plans. Output clean top view, floorplate and dollhouse, link same dimensions to facade window grid. Hero Type A detailed: believable furniture, circulation, fitted cabinetry, bathroom fixtures, kitchen joinery, balcony railing. Type B visually distinct at moderate fidelity. No invisible room geometry posing as complete room.
P4 Two amenity floors: wellness with gym equipment, reception, pool deck, water plane and plausible plant/service zone; upper dining with kitchen, service, café counters, tables and separate shisha terrace zone subject to feasibility. Provide 2D fallback exploration and at least one premium rendered scene per amenity.
P5 Physically based materials: 0-1 roughness/metalness, consistent UV scales, linear/sRGB texture correctness, orientation-specific normals, tile joint scale and wood grain direction. Validate tone mapping and hue matches across Blender and three.js (visually close; exact physically identical not assumed). Separate editable slots/mesh names and keep all swatches standardized.
P6 Lighting: exterior morning/afternoon and evening; interior warm/cool fixture layers, plausible daylight through actual apertures, contact shadows, AO judiciously, reflection probes/environment/HDRI if licensed, avoid light leaks and blown highlights. Consistent white balance, realistic bloom only if restrained.
P7 Animated cameras: define exact key camera bookmarks with target/orbit boundaries, 3D coordinate map, near/far/FOV, transition paths, easing; avoid geometry collision; matching 2D fade/cut fallback. Export original cinematic stills/screenshots and separate gallery renders.
P8 Export: .blend original source and reproducible Blender Python, metric geometry, Y-up conversion for GLB, consistent origin and pivots, LODs where necessary, GLB/glTF 2.0 scenes grouped logically (exterior, repeated level, hero unit, amenities), keep semantic object/material names where raycasting/editing uses them. Use tools such as glTF Transform only if verified trusted. Compress geometry/textures as supported, KTX2/Basis if beneficial and compatible, Draco/Meshopt when measured; ensure required decoders available and version matched. Verify in real browser.
P9 Optimization: instancing repetitive windows/balcony parts, texture atlases when material editability not broken, drawcall budgets derived from measured devices, preload first view only, lazy-load interior/amenities, asset unmount/disposal policy, control DPR and shadows by tier, on-demand rendering when no animation, avoid new material object on every click, no memory leak on switching floors repeatedly.
P10 Visual QA: compare Blender hero still vs browser screenshot from same camera direction, matching facade band, floor count, accent materials and window positions. Artifact tracking: preview render, viewport test, material slot list, GLB inspector report, texture bytes, drawcalls, fps, memory. Fix obvious intersections, clipping, repeated textures, unrealistic scale, reflections and light leaks. Only then proceed.

## Capture design
Each hero still must have composition notes: focal point, 35mm-ish/other chosen FOV, horizon, time of day, camera height and target. Shot schedule: (01) exterior three-quarter golden hour, (02) front daylight architectural, (03) evening facade, (04) lobby, (05) cutaway typical floor, (06) unit A dollhouse, (07) living wide, (08) kitchen 3/4, (09) bedroom, (10) bathroom, (11) balcony, (12) pool, (13) gym, (14) wellness lounge, (15) rooftop café, (16) restaurant, (17) separate terrace. A scene photograph can be a render; never pass it off as actual built property photo. Respect visual consistency, color grading and appropriate sizes/compression.

## Material and image synchronization
Create a package snapshot matrix: e.g. Warm Ivory Standard, Walnut Premium and Pearl Signature. Pre-render chosen canonical schemes. Runtime change only modifies validated targeted scene materials and finish board. Switching to a photo gallery after arbitrary custom mix triggers notice or a generated true scene capture; do NOT represent a mismatched pre-render as exact current selection. Consider screenshot export at current WebGL camera for real state, with disclaimers about lighting/quality differences.

## Device tiers and fallback
Tier A: capable WebGL, full building + accurate room inspector (real materials).
Tier B: reduced geometry/textures/shadows/quality tier, still fully interactive floor navigation and selected materials.
Tier C: no WebGL, 2D floor map, gallery and material info/side-by-side pre-render packages; customization clearly marked as limited preview; all basic commercial actions available.
Choose numeric target budgets *after real profiling*. Baseline launch targets (not promises): Lighthouse performance >=80 desktop and >=60 mobile, accessibility >=90 where measurable, LCP <=2.5s on representative fast conditions, CLS <=0.1, responsiveness close to instant for material switch, 30fps+ on average mobile during static exploration where tested. If targets missed, show actual metric and remediation.

## Asset production acceptance gate
An asset passes only if (a) no license ambiguity, (b) semantically named, (c) dimensions/source-of-truth aligned, (d) realistic enough in target shots, (e) GLB import works, (f) interactive selection works, (g) memory/cpu acceptable, (h) fallback exists or error state describes limitation. Never call primitive boxes with flat colors "photorealistic completion".

DOCUMENT: specs/MATERIAL_CONFIGURATOR.md
===========================================================================
# Interactive materials + finish package specification

## Core data and semantics
Materials are a normalized domain: catalog item, supplier metadata (verified vs fictional), swatches, PBR map URLs, physical use restrictions, version/date, area/units, provenance/license, description bilingual, pricing (optional/unknown), and BIM/material Passport-style exported note. Each editable surface in a scene has a stable unique ID and allowed category. Available materials are filtered by approved application (e.g. moisture/slip exposure). The scene's base option must be explicit and restored on reset.

## Six v1 categories, 3 variants each (18 minimum)
1. Flooring: warm ivory porcelain tile matte, pearl marble-look porcelain (label imitation, not real marble), engineered oak (dry living areas only); wet/outdoor restrictions explicit.
2. Wall paint: warm cream, pearl soft grey, muted sage; distinct roughness and surface finish, trim coordination.
3. Wood panels/finishes: natural oak veneer-look, medium walnut veneer-look, dark walnut veneer-look. Avoid inventing FSC certification or real supplier.
4. Kitchen cabinet fronts: matte ivory, warm walnut veneer-look, charcoal matte; appliance/handle colors remain coherent.
5. Countertops: white quartz-like engineered stone, dark granite-look, ivory marble-look; distinguish look from real mineral composition; avoid false durability or certification claims.
6. Bathroom tiles: warm stone-look grip finish, satin grey porcelain, white marble-look porcelain; explicit floor/wall eligibility in sample database.

## Context interactions
- Hover/focus on eligible mesh displays small outlined highlight+name. Click/Enter selects surface and opens inspector with data and current sample. Avoid blanket highlighting noneditable objects. Selected material persists when switching view, unit, language or day/night.
- Alternative selector includes thumbnail, label, optional manufacturer/spec details if verified, application safety notes, and 3D live preview. Hover preview optional and reversible, click commits selection. Show real physical UV scale and proper normal/roughness updates, not just mesh tint.
- Live material swap must have undo, redo, reset one surface, reset room, reset unit, package preset, compare, save, export. With dozens of surfaces, use batching; avoid excessive React rerenders and expensive shader recompiles.
- Package selection updates multiple scoped slots atomically, preserving possibility to override individual selections. UI announces overridden material differences; no surprises.
- Material names and specs show demonstration labels unless actually sourced, never use arbitrary invented brand, country of origin, slip rating, fire class, warranty or price as facts.
- A/B compare uses stable same viewpoint, same time-of-day and exposure, draggable wipe or side-by-side; accessible non-drag toggle. Screenshot export must include visible statement "3D visual concept / materials are illustrative".

## Technical architecture
Store catalog separate from scene file; IDs unify Three mesh userData.surfaceId and backend record keys. Use reducer/store commands rather than ephemeral component local state; state shape (project, floor, unit, room, selected surface, package, surfaceOverrides, history, comparison, locale). Serialize a small versioned share payload; validate ID allowlist and bounds, never blindly deserialize client data. Material clones/scoped assignments avoid changing surfaces in every apartment because shared GLB material mutated by reference.
- Distinguish per-room material applied to wall vs floor via surface classes. Expose excluded choices disabled with explanatory reasons.
- Material quality: correct color spaces (color albedo sRGB, normal/roughness non-color), tiling coordinates and real tile widths where possible, visible grout scale, credible edge textures; texture normal map gamma not corrupted.
- Texture loading and caching: preload 3 starter swatches; lazily load other variants, show pending indicator, keep old material if load fails, allow retry. Reuse decoded textures carefully and release on scene disposal.

## Material passport/export
Unit A Floor 04, design timestamp and demo disclaimer, full scene selections by ID, supplier metadata status, included/excluded room surfaces, conceptual image preview, alternatives and optional budget placeholders. Layout Arabic RTL and English, clickable room/material index. PDF generated locally if library exists or print-to-PDF fallback. Do not claim it is a legal warranty, bill of materials or purchase agreement.

## Failure cases
Re-render a room and ensure material persists; undo after changing package; refresh and restore (opt-in); entering unavailable unit; corrupted shared URL; undefined catalog ID; mesh missing surface mapping; failed texture; switching day/night; language toggle; mobile gestures while material pane is open; user returning to gallery after custom mix. Each must have deterministic behavior and/or honest fallback.

DOCUMENT: specs/AMENITY_AND_SAFETY.md

# High-value amenities and architectural risk register

## Level 10: Wellness and recreational level
Program to test, not fixed sizing: welcome/reception, comfortable fitness room, strength/cardio/studio zones, swimming pool/deck, separated changing rooms + showers + sanitary facilities, sauna/steam if economically/regulatorily viable, relaxation lounge, towel/linen/storage, accessible WC and route, safety equipment, janitor/plant/humidity/dehumidification/service zones. Operationally separate wet circulation from gym/restaurant traffic and private residential routes.
Critical unknowns: actual pool dimensions and depth, load, vibration, transfer structure, drainage and leak detection, water treatment, enclosed pool HVAC and vapor barrier/condensation, acoustic transmission, rescue rules, slip-resistant decks, fire separation, occupancy/evacuation, accessible arrival, maintenance access. No unqualified structural load estimates asserted as approved. Show a "structural and MEP engineering review required" note in planning artifacts. If elevated pool proves physically or commercially unrealistic, autonomously evaluate moving it to a low podium or roof-adjacent pool and document ADR; preserve a wow-factor guided 3D tour.
Digital wow-factor: raymarched-like or physically credible water shader if performant, gentle caustics or baked effects, controlled reflections, separation of visible glass and steam, purposeful camera anchors, selectable equipment information cards, facilities gallery. For indoor pool, no unrealistic giant open-air infinity edge if not structurally resolved.

## Level 11: Sky restaurant, café, shisha and terrace
Program: restaurant indoor dining, kitchen with separated service zones (fire and grease), café counter + seating, private panoramic lounge, restrooms, storage, waste, service corridor, manager/host, open-air terrace. Shisha lounge conditional: study jurisdictional legality, licensing, smoking restrictions and whether adult-only controlled area could be permitted, combustion/CO risks, separation from nonsmoking patrons and residential fresh-air intakes. Do not claim mechanical ventilation completely eliminates secondhand smoke exposure. Model as a separate open-air zone with clear signage and no access for minors, or swap concept to non-smoking panoramic lounge if constraints fail. Food/health/fire approvals are outside website model scope.
- Need independent guest/resident access concept, noise control, evening atmosphere without oversaturating lights, weather protection, fall protection and service logistics. Rooftop restaurants need real mechanical and wastewater routing; render believable ducts/plant enclosures at architectural scale.
- UI: switch restaurant/café/terrace zones, time-of-day, gallery carousel, information only. Reservation widget, menu/payment or real contact should be demo-only unless a business owner supplies real backend integration.

## Safety boundaries
This is a fictional real-estate marketing showcase, not signed-off architectural drawings. All safety constraints are research questions and code-compliance gates. No "certified safe", "building approved", "available for sale", "actual photograph", "compliant pool", "legal shisha" claims without evidence. Red flags go to risks register and design alternative, not hidden in pretty visuals.

## Commercial value
The amenities deliver 3D tour variety, differ from repetitive apartments, help real estate company demos of lifestyle. However they should not consume 80% of the initial build time at expense of one excellent exterior and unit. Sequence: functional zones -> one scene quality -> polished later.
