Store independent fields: environmentPresetId, timeMinutes [0..1439], seasonId, weatherId, simulatedDateISO, geoReferenceZone (Basra region, approximate), ambientSoundEnabled, environmentEffectsEnabled. Locale and units separate. Changes must be stable across all exploration views; changing environment does NOT reset camera, material overrides, scene, floor, room, selected unit, favorites, view mode or accessibility preferences. URL share may include compact environment tokens. Invalid shared values yield safe defaults, not app crash. Changing seasons while walking inside is not forced to reinitialize collision or wall geometry.

## Scientific/visual correspondence
Use coherent geographical approximate sun direction based on a notional geographic orientation; a floor plan must declare north in the canonical data model. To simulate realistic sun, implement/test a solar positioning algorithm with local date, Baghdad timezone, broad Basra coordinates where appropriate; label as illustrative if lacking a real project address or accurate site north. If physically accurate implementation is not viable, use visually plausible documented solar presets; avoid marketing it as an engineered daylight or energy study. Indoor light through physically modeled windows should change with sun side and exposure; light intensity and shade under fins/balconies should behave believably. Night: darker sky, minimal indirect ambience, correctly lit interior/window emissives and restrained architecture lighting. Dawn/dusk: warm low-angle sun, long shadows and graded sky. Cloudy: softer diffuse shadows, desaturated contrast, lower direct sunlight. Rain: darkens **exterior** pavement and stone roughness appropriately, low-cost puddle decals where feasible, restrained sky/drizzle/streaks, NO synthetic rain in furnished apartment unless door open. Dust: warm neutral haze, distant visibility reduction, particles optional, safety/comfort not obscured. Wind: subtle tree motion in higher tiers, no wind physics demands on buildings.
For interior changes, daylight and blinds/shading interact; material colors must not permanently change on environment toggle. PBR materials preserve own hue/roughness; sky/HDRI affects lighting only. The material inspector always displays actual chosen category, not weather-dependent fake color name. Night lamps use tagged emissive circuits and tuned light budget, not dozens of realtime shadow-casting points.

## Performance strategy: NO EXCEPTION
No full 12-building copies for each season, no 4 video overlays on mobile, no loading all environment HDRIs/8K skies upfront. Parameterize the SAME geometry; use few small shared sky/cubemap/HDRI variants with lighting interpolation and atmospheric shaders that can be simplified. Keep rain/wind/dust effects on a toggleable plugin/subscene, with per-quality object/particle caps. Static 2D fallback illustrates day/night/season/weather via compressed responsive artwork, with honest labels. Strongly prefer 2-4 cheap sky texture variants rather than 24 preloaded 4K HDR maps.
- Update only relevant shader uniforms, sun+sky and specific terrain/wetness channels on slider movement; debounce heavier shadow map changes while dragging, apply final high-quality state on release.
- Smooth 250-800ms transition (actual time profiled) for most changes, preserving accessibility reduced-motion instant alternative.
- Stop simulation at idle, offscreen, background tab or low quality. Never run particle animation if 2D Lite view is active.
- Prefetch only adjacent environment assets when network spare capacity exists; environmental changes NEVER delay floor selection or room/back buttons.
- Automatic quality manager may disable animated rain/vegetation while retaining *visible weather state* in sky/lighting and selected label.

## Weather room effects and view consistency
Exterior and balcony see sky/weather, while interior primarily gets window illumination and distant atmospherics. When a visitor is inside Living Room on floor 04 and switches to night+rain+winter, camera stays on floor04/living; external windows become night/rain, room lamps activate (depending on controlled switch), floor/wood materials remain unchanged. Interior gallery high-quality stills must be labeled as a preset if it doesn't match custom current time/material/weather. Never silently state gallery reflects a combination not actually rendered. `Capture this view` should use current viewer pixels (quality disclosure), while `Premium Render Gallery` explicitly shows curated shots from Blender.

## UX narrative & use cases
- Exterior example: Afternoon clear Summer -> sunset Spring -> rainy winter evening -> warm summer dawn, all without scene reload.
- Floor example: choose Floor 07 then change from day to night; individual apartment balcony is shaded according to facade orientation.
- Room example: kitchen walnut to pearl-white, then sun from 14:00 to 18:00 and partly cloudy without losing the finish; sharing reproduces both choices.
- Balcony example: building north compass, approximate solar angle; do not invent a real river view or neighboring skyline.
- Amenity: pool and restaurant vary mood; pool glazing sunset, café night lighting. Water reflections use efficient approximation and static baked details on lower tiers; no expensive full real-time refraction required.

## UI semantics/labels
`Simulated Environment`, `Summer / صيف`, `Light Rain / مطر خفيف`, `Golden Hour / الساعة الذهبية`, `Sun direction / اتجاه الشمس`, `Current design selection preserved`. If "current weather" offered in the future, it must be a clearly distinct opt-in integration tied to a real reliable source; user consent, rate limits, fallback and no hidden paid API. Tooltip on sun compass: "Visual concept only, not a verified solar/thermal study".

## Test matrix P0
1. All 4 seasons and six weather states selectable, no fatal errors; 2. All times 00:00, 06:00, 12:00, 18:00, 23:59 and slider continuous; 3. After 100 environment switches scene does not leak; 4. material selection identical before/after switch; 5. no 3D scene re-fetch for base model after weather change; 6. no autoplay sound; 7. atmosphere state persists on navigation, reload and locale change; 8. correct outdoor vs indoor effects, no indoor rain; 9. performance budget maintained by fallback on low; 10. accessible labels and reduced-motion; 11. 2D mode shows approximate weather state; 12. invalid share query safely resets; 13. no false live weather claim; 14. visually validate 4 representative combinations with screenshots; 15. phone rotation/keyboard switching does not reset environment; 16. hover/click floors unaffected by rain effect mesh; 17. camera unaffected while environment transitions.

Potential future WOW: accurately simulated sun-path and shadow on selected balcony after professional orientation review, downloadable lighting comparison snapshots for buyer, quiet ambient audio on opt-in with licensing, guided seasonal tours. These are separate milestones after performance passes.


--- specs/FREE_WALK_CAMERA_AND_WAYFINDING.md ---
# FREE WALK & CAMERA GRAMMAR — FROM STREET TO EVERY ACCESSIBLE ROOM

## Required user promise
The user wants to physically enter the building, floors, rooms and amenities and **walk/look around freely** like a premium guided architectural experience. A row of disconnected still images, auto-spinning panorama or cut to a static image is not an acceptable substitute for this feature. Implement actual navigable 3D spaces for the hero unit and all showcased amenity areas; other units may share consistent template geometry at appropriate fidelity, explicitly documented as representative rather than 18 truly unique authored interiors. The user should be able to discover all 12 levels via DOM controls, including vertical movement to amenity floors, and free-walk the rooms provided in the implemented 3D plans. The existence of a room in the schedule MUST map to a safe view mode even if detailed furnishing is not finished; don't falsely pretend a still is freewalk.

## Modes (one camera state machine, not conflicting controls)
A. `Cinematic Tour`: optional auto-directed overview; skippable, interruptible, accessible, separate from free movement. A short tour stops smoothly at exterior, floor, dollhouse, living, material inspector, wellness, pool and rooftop café; if assets missing branch to documented fallback, not broken camera animation.
B. `Orbit Exterior`: constrained orbit around tower, zoom bounds, smooth inertia, pan limit, no below-ground camera, prevents clipping through entire tower accidentally; floor raycast and semantic list synchronized.
C. `Floor Cutaway`: exterior zoom aligns to selected level, floor plate rises/isolates, wall/shell fades or hides without changing floor IDs; user orbits floor plan, chooses unit A/B, then a specific room.
D. `Dollhouse`: 3D overhead tilted furnished unit with walls cut/roof removed selectively. Room hotspots and labels are accessible DOM controls and clickable 3D targets; coherent floorplan and door placement.
E. `Guided Interior`: default easy mode; tap room labels/map -> smoothly teleport to sensible camera bookmarks with optional short walk transition. Works great on mobile without joystick frustration.
F. `Free Walk`: opt-in first-person continuous movement inside real modeled accessible geometry; look around, step through openable doors, visit adjacent rooms, discover window/balcony; keep camera from clipping/moving through solid walls. User may exit walk mode instantly, jump to selected room, or press Home.
G. `Accessibility/Lite`: semantic plans, material panels, labelled photos with full equivalent information; no promises of actual 3D movement if hardware lacks WebGL.

## Walk and physics requirements
Use documented camera eye height starting ~1.60-1.70m with accessible preset variations, realistic walk speed (~1-1.6m/s baseline) with user control, limited acceleration and damping, zero head bob by default, no camera roll. Use navmesh + swept collision or robust simple capsule collider, static wall meshes and door passage tags. Do not implement expensive full physics for furniture when simple collision proxies suffice. Avoid walking through walls, bed, glass, handrails, pool water, floors, furniture or closed doors; ensure floating/falling out of world cannot occur. Staircase/corridor collision treated consistently; for 12-level traversal, **accessible elevator menu/teleport** is preferred over demanding users physically climb stairs. Actual elevator animation may be a shallow optional transition but its choices must be navigable, matching floor list. Do not let a 3D character fall from balcony/roof; barriers and safe bounds needed.
- Room positions derived from canonical room polygons/nav graph; collision bounds and eye-level camera anchors match geometry; no entering mechanically inaccessible rooms or ceiling voids. For impossible narrow spaces, provide orbit/bookmark view rather than fake walk. Restricted plant/services zones marked not publicly explorable.
- Door interactions: on click/tap or approach prompt, rotate around a real pivot and maintain physical clearance; door never blocks navmesh without recalculation or state. Disable door opening effect if it causes clipping, default to open door. Transparent glass should be visually detectable; collision frame should match.
- Stairs/elevator: ensure accessible and coherent on concept plans. Elevator menu opens a list G,01..11 and highlights current level; entering selected floor never loads all floor interiors. If user clicks stair and no full 3D stair traversal, show honest transition to next level, not fake functional staircase.
- Walk input: WASD/arrows desktop, dragging mouse for look only after user engages walkthrough (pointer lock with clear exit if used), gamepad optional and not mandatory; touch virtual dual-stick is optional with polished design, tap-to-move/waypoint essential mobile fallback; thumb joystick must not obscure UI, adjustable sensitivity. Prevent keyboard shortcuts during forms. Treat desktop touchscreens as hybrid.
- Tap-to-move: Raycast navigable floor, select target waypoint, highlight path and ease camera to the point using validated navigation; no teleport behind closed door or into walls. Long press/gesture aim should not hijack browser navigation. For very weak devices prefer fixed camera anchor and smart room-hop instead of stutter.

## Navigation map and route grammar
A persistent miniature plan with current position, compass, entry direction, doors, and room labels; selectable destinations include Living, Kitchen, Master Bedroom, Second Bedroom, Bath, Balcony, Lobby, Gym, Indoor Pool, Sauna, Cafe, Restaurant and Terrace as appropriate. Map toggles accessible; on small phones a floating `Map` icon opens bottom sheet. Breadcrumb example: Nakhla Residence > Floor 04 > Unit 04A > Kitchen > Countertop. Buttons Back / Exit Walk / Floor List / Home remain visible and work after refresh/deep link. Ensure high-value demo never takes more than ~2 clear navigation choices from overview to living, and from current room to material inspector (measure during UX testing).

## Floor/unit room completeness and truthfulness
- 01-09 are real selectable floors, with coherent A/B locations. All 18 apartment selections show floor/unit data and at minimum an actual navigable minimal geometry using shared type templates, plus room navigation; if only hero home detailed, label others `Representative layout preview` and refrain from unique furnishing/photorealism claims. Better a common parametric geometry with varied orientation and views than 18 disconnected fake screenshots.
- F10 gym, pool and recreation must include actual interactive 3D walkable floor area; designate pool edge barriers and no swimming simulation; water material scalable/reflection alternate. F11 restaurant/cafe/terrace must be physically navigable, including separate (conditional) shisha area, usable furniture paths and safe railings. If constraints demand limited interiors, scope release honestly and never show inactive controls pretending a scene exists.
- Exterior path from street: camera can focus/drop-off, approach lobby, navigate lobby area with actual interior surfaces, optionally enter elevator to Floor 04; users can also jump directly from floor picker. The handoff should feel continuous without forcing one huge 12-story model resident in GPU memory.

## Camera transition director
Store cameraAnchor IDs per floor/room and semantic entrance/exit target; each transition has `from`, `to`, duration range, easing, collision-safe bezier/spline or direct fade, and cancellation/resume. Never animate camera through walls/furniture just because a mathematical curve looks stylish. For distant levels use short transition with tasteful cut/fade, preserving spatial orientation via mini map and floor labels. Sync UI navigation state only after scene target is ready, with reliable rollback on load errors. Use per-gesture cancellation tokens. For reduced motion, instantly switch to target frame and remove background animations. Frame budget manager may downgrade post effects during camera movement to maintain FPS, then improve on idle.

## Scene object interaction
Raycast priority: material-surface hotspots if inspector enabled > room target hotspot > floor selection > decoration; do not let weather particles, glass panes or invisible shadow meshes intercept clicks. Hit targets with enlarged bounds on touch; provide consistent hover/selected states and 2D button equivalence. Selecting carpet/wood/wall shows exact scene surface ID, catalog metadata and actual selected material. A wrong room surface is a P0 bug. On transitions, close panels that are scene-specific while preserving changed finishes and providing undo. Camera must not jump on scrolling info drawer. User can return from Material Inspector to exactly previous camera pose.

## Camera artifact deliverables
- `NAV_GRAPH.json` with rooms/floors/door adjacency, collision & camera anchor IDs.
- `CAMERA_BOOKMARKS.json`, `SHOT_LIST.md`, optional guided timeline JSON, movement control manual for touch/mouse/keyboard.
- Screen capture/video of real 3D freewalk through Living -> Kitchen -> Hall -> Master (as applicable) AND F10 Gym->Pool AND F11 Café->Terrace; these must be actual browser captures, not Blender flythrough represented as interactive.
- QA report: collision tests, 15 rapid route selections, 10 enter/exit walk cycles, camera loss after tab resume, device rotate, reduced motion, missing model and fallback.

## Release gates
No deployment as full freewalk demo if selected walk mode is a fake still, user cannot leave walk mode, floor selection becomes stale, camera passes through solid wall/ground, mobile person can't move to an adjacent room, navigation state resets on weather/quality changes, or amenity zones crash. A labelled guided-room alternative is acceptable on low-end devices and must preserve content parity.


--- specs/WOW_FEATURES_AND_PRODUCT_STRATEGY_V2.md ---
# WORLD-CLASS WOW FEATURES — MEANINGFUL, OPTIONAL, BUDGETED

## Strategic goal
The result should make a property developer say "I want my project presented this way" while still being easy for an ordinary client who opened a link in WhatsApp. WOW is not hundreds of fireworks or random UI motion; it is useful interactive spatial storytelling, personalized finishes and emotional but tasteful presentation. Every additional feature needs a demonstrable sales/clarity benefit, measurable performance cost, accessibility alternative, original/licensed assets and a graceful off switch. Never inflate scope so the hero experience remains half-built.

## TIER 0: always complete before experimental extras
1. Cinematic 3-second-to-skip intro using static poster immediately and GPU scene only when ready; gorgeous but optional.
2. Real 3D tower and 12-level browser with selector list mirrored in DOM.
3. Select apartment A/B, see accurate cutaway, enter hero Living/Kitchen/Master/Bath/Balcony, walk/guided room hop.
4. Examine actual material details + changing PBR surface live + finish packages + undo/reset.
5. Visit Gym/Pool Level 10 and Café/Restaurant Level 11 with actual reliable 3D navigation.
6. Set time, weather and season; state preserved; suitable mobile Lite mode.
7. Beautiful original, coherent render gallery + information + print/export and truthful demo inquiry.
8. Consistent good performance under mobile constraints, bilingual responsive/accessibility and stable link.

## TIER 1: strong differentiation, add only after baseline passes
A. **Virtual Architect / Guided Explorer**: short optional story chapters, move through building, floor plan, room, materials, amenities, return to summary. Each chapter has precise skip/stop and translated copy. Can create a personalized demo route from currently selected unit, no generative API/hidden costs.
B. **Solar & Shade Discovery**: `Where does the sun reach my apartment?` uses notional site north and time/season and explicitly simulated shadows; let buyer observe balcony shade and sunlit room, with a notice that it is conceptual rather than a certified energy study. Adds value specifically for hot-climate Basra.
C. **Instant Before / After / Package Split**: compare Standard vs Premium or original vs personalized finishes by smart material change not duplicate high-poly scene; usable slider accessible on keyboard; when scene cost high, capture A screenshot then B and compare 2 stills with honest labels.
D. **Building X-Ray / Peel-Away**: facade gradually becomes translucent/floors separate to show shared corridors and apartment location. Never render all furnished 18 homes simultaneously; simplify underlying interiors. Optional `Infrastructure Reveal` later only if drawings exist.
E. **True Perspective View from This Floor**: select floor, camera aligns to approximate balcony viewpoint and shows a fictional Basra-inspired skyline aligned to notional orientations. DO NOT fake a view of an actual neighborhood/river as factual. Add `Compass/North` and illustrative sunlight.
F. **My Apartment Collection**: visitor favorites options, side-by-side compare 2 floor types, bookmark room + camera + environment, share compact view link and print-to-PDF selection cards. Privacy-friendly local state by default, no signup wall.
G. **Material Passport QR / Smart Spec Board**: downloadable no-brand-infringement finish board with catalog IDs, actual metadata source and fictional concept stamp, side-by-side color chips and suggested performance properties only if verified; no invented manufacturer certifications.
H. **One-Click Cinematic Screenshot**: capture user's current 3D view and an optional captioned marketing image. On low tier capture at current render quality, not fake 8K; no uploading camera/frame to server unless opted in.
I. **Salesperson Presentation Mode**: one button hides dev UI, shows simplified floor selector and prepared 5-minute walkthrough; offline or prefetch mode documented, no automatic pub. Allows buyer to step in at any time.
J. **Design Pulse**: tap a room to see count of selected finish alternatives and category status; visually subtle, no gamification distracting from purchase.

## TIER 2: future paid product possibilities (do not block MVP)
- Editable project CMS where clients import plans, BLEND/GLB, finish catalogs and gallery; validation creates floor/unit/surface mapping.
- Studio-level renders generated on server with a queue (costed and opt-in) matching custom finishes exactly, not fake real-time instant GPU ray tracing on mobile.
- CRM inquiry, scheduled tours, property availability sync, lead permissions, authorized company admin.
- Accurate BIM/IFC import, MEP/service cutaways, clash review and spec traceability by licensed architect/engineer.
- Multi-unit compare with mortgage/financing, price calculation and standardized sale documents only with real developer data and approvals.
- VR/WebXR when hardware supports it and reviewed security/accessibility; no forced headset.
- Home furnishing configurator with SKU/licensing and delivery integration only after clear agreement.
- Optional real weather API, precise solar study, construction time-lapse from legitimate media, measured sound insulation and energy score — NEVER fabricate metrics.
- Optional AI sales assistant restricted to documented project facts and transparent limitations, only if business value outweighs runtime/API/privacy costs.

## Distinctive hero scenarios the developer can demo
1. User opens link on a midrange phone; within short wait sees responsive hero and floor picker even as 3D downloads. Taps Floor 04 -> A -> Living; changes porcelain to oak. No spinner trap, no jump. 2 minutes.
2. On an iPad, shows 10 Wellness; moves inside Gym, navigates to pool; changes environment to Sunset + Winter Light Rain; reflection/sky changes appropriately, pool never receives exterior rain. 1 minute.
3. On a laptop, use Daylight Detective: point compass to afternoon summer sun, examine balcony shade, compare against golden-hour spring, identify sunlit side. 1 minute.
4. On desktop, one-click guided cinematic full tour; pause to freely explore master bedroom, inspect walnut sample, save report, generate demo inquiry (locally only). 2 minutes.
5. On a low-power phone or WebGL unsupported: click `Lite`, select floor, see plan and representative stills, inspect material spec and receive honest PDF — full commercial narrative still accessible.

## UI style / restraint
Modern Iraqi Luxury: warm-ivory stone, sophisticated bronze accents, dark charcoal typography or optional dark theme, Arabic-first identity, generous breathing room, refined motion, high-quality iconography, tactile photos, clear interactions. Luxury does NOT mean unreadable tiny text or overloaded glass-morphism. Add no looping video background, no autoplay audio, no massive particle weather effect, no invasive popups, no deceptive synthetic neighborhood claims. Design and proof with actual screenshot comparisons.

## Hard priority override
If any TIER 1 feature causes mobile regression, downgrade it or defer; if any TIER 2 proposal requires credits, licenses, external accounts or user data, document as future optional — DO NOT install/run/pay by default. Do not claim 100% completion if future ideas were intentionally scoped out; label what is ready vs roadmap.


--- qa/PERFORMANCE_E2E_ACCEPTANCE.md ---
# REPRODUCIBLE DEVICE & PERFORMANCE RELEASE GATES — V2

## What counts as tested
Every result needs date, local browser/version, OS, device model or hardware class, viewport/CSS size, actual DPR, selected quality tier, network shaping, cold/warm cache, scene ID, FPS summary, network transfer, logs, pass/fail, screenshot/trace file paths. Tests in mocked/emulated browser must be explicitly labelled; mobile emulation does not replace a real iPhone/Android. CI results are not automatically user-experience proof. `NOT_RUN` is not a pass. A link being reachable on the author's PC is not proof it works for a remote recipient.

## Release process
1. Validate source fixture and asset manifest, check safe versions/license, run typecheck/lint/unit tests; failures block review.
2. Smoke load served production build locally, not dev hot module app; critical brand content visible with 3D disabled; no console errors or broken network requests; baseline JS/CSS/poster initial transfer recorded.
3. Run representative automated E2E on Chromium, Firefox and WebKit engine if available, keyboard and mobile viewport, Arabic and English. Use at least one actual iPhone Safari and Android Chrome if accessible, iPad if possible; document unverified combinations explicitly.
4. Measure Core Web Vitals and 3D scene metrics **separately** with 3G/4G network simulation and device CPU throttle. Lighthouse can be used but do not invent a composite target as a substitute for specific metrics. Aim >=90 performance score as stretch, not an unsupported pass/fail guarantee. Confirm LCP <=2.5s / INP <=200ms / CLS <=0.1 on field p75 where field data exists; for no field data only report lab proxies and measured values.
5. Stress-test: 10 floor jumps F01/F09/F04/F10/F11/G sequences; 20 legal material swaps; 12 weather/time/season changes; 10 enter/exit walk cycles; 3 complete presentation loops; rapid navigation back/forward, deep link reload, lost network during GLB fetch; repeated tab hiding/resuming.
6. Enter low-tier forced QA; intentionally disable WebGL, kill network, simulate missing GLB/decoder, call WebGL loseContext in a controlled test, break one texture; app stays usable with Lite and reports recoverable state, no blank canvas.
7. Inspect responsive screenshots at matrix widths and portrait/landscape, device zoom 125% and phone font scale, Arabic/English. Actual touch gestures verified. Test orientation change during material change and in walk mode; no crash or state reset.
8. Visual acceptance pass: facade proportions match floor cutaway; floor pick maps correctly, actual material changes vs spec, sky position plausible, exterior rain does not intrude indoor, dawn/day/sunset/night renders comparable; scenes sufficiently attractive after optimization.
9. Prioritize fixes: P0 blockers => no showcase release; P1 major => fix or documented scope reduction before external company link; P2 cosmetic => backlog acceptable with evidence.
10. Final preview, build artifacts, source Blender/GLB, manifest with rights, screenshots, performance evidence, browser compatibility table, Git status/commit, RESUME_HERE and honest delivered-vs-not-delivered summary.

## Example P0 scripted user journey (must be real, not generated assertions)
- Cold open `/ar/explore` on shaped mid-range mobile; title and button display independently of GLB; tap explore; select F04 via list/3D; select 04A; open living; enter free walk or low-tier guided scene; select floor material; change sample; confirm rendered GPU/material changed; change to sunset/rain/winter; confirm floor material and camera preserved; open photo gallery with honest labels; save/share print report; switch English; reload direct link; exit. Validate every step and capture evidence.
- Cold open `/en/explore` on desktop, mouse select F10 and actual gym/pool movement; choose F11 and actual restaurant/terrace movement; environment mode day->night, open finish board; then hit browser back, restore correct floor; no WebGL exceptions.
- Force no-WebGL on iPad-sized viewport: floor/unit selection, room photos, material info, compare and export still work.

## Robustness edge-case grid
Smallest phones, orientation change, high zoom, dynamic safe area, slow response, connection 404/500, WebGL context lost and restored, page backgrounding, invalid user shared URL, unknown surface ID, missing texture, locale missing key, alternate materials racing, old cached code vs new model, tab reload during asset fetch, accidental double taps, language switch while moving, rapid top-level scene choice, ARIA keyboard-only access, reduced motion, sound off and denied motion permission.

## Strict release decision template
`Feature / target` | `Observed result` | `Test context` | `Evidence` | `Risk/severity` | `Action` | `Owner` | `Final status`.
No fabricated 'all green'. If some device wasn't tested explicitly say 'unverified'. If one experimental WOW feature misses a budget, disable on low/mid or ship behind opt-in flag; do not sacrifice baseline features. A polished, well-measured hybrid demo with a truthful limitation is preferable to a supposedly flawless website that crashes on a user's phone.


--- FILE: specs/V3_PRIORITY_OVERRIDES_AND_FIXES.md ---
# V3 HIGHEST-PRIORITY REQUIREMENTS / CONFLICT RESOLUTION

Order of precedence: safety, user-stated 12-level building + contact details, functional correctness, link-share reliability, graceful performance, accessibility & calm UX, architectural coherence, premium visual quality, cinematic extras, other roadmap ideas. Quality never excuses broken navigation. V3 docs supersede conflicting V1/V2 claims; requirements from prior versions still apply where compatible.

## Audit-identified consistency corrections
1. Old descriptions sometimes say '10 floors' from the initial idea. AUTHORITATIVE floor stack is **G + F01–F09 residential + F10 wellness/pool + F11 sky dining = 12 occupied levels**. 18 apartments (2 per F01–F09); F10/F11 have NO apartments. Seed model is concept not site-ready construction.
2. 'Double-height lobby' must not silently remove a slice of F01 residential floor. Either make grand lobby a single-volume tall ground floor, or explicitly redesign the adjacent portion of F01 and reconcile 18-unit program. Default to a tall G lobby with convincing height rather than an inconsistent spatial void.
3. F11 sky dining and an outdoor terrace is treated as the 12th occupied level; roof maintenance/service above is not an extra occupancy floor. If shisha legality, zoning or smoke control cannot be confirmed, keep an *optional concept* off-by-default outdoor zone with no claim of legal operation.
4. Pool on F10 is merely architectural concept: heavy water loads, waterproofing, dampness, mechanical plant, evacuation, structural/MEP/fire design need specialist sign-off and potentially a lower location in a real commission. Do not invent an approved roof swimming pool.
5. Screenshot from old reference reel is inspiration only, not authorized product artwork. Reference media stays only inside local design research. Original authored site renders, textures, screenshots and code only in public output.
6. No 'no glitches for all devices' or 'zero bugs' guarantee. Every tested claim names hardware, browser, network and build version; untested marked explicitly. For unsupported GPU/browser, show informative full-function Lite experience. Adopt budgets that are measurable, prioritized and tuned to real hardware.
7. Scope of 'all rooms' means all semantically selectable and truthfully represented; fully navigable 3D for modeled hero spaces and amenities; reduced-detail/2D for unmodeled scenes. Do not imply 18 distinct bespoke interiors if only Type A/B reuse.
8. Landscape render/photo consistency: preset renders match their labeled finish packages; modified custom settings use a current-scene snapshot (with caveat fidelity) or explicit illustrative caption. Never label stale image as exact changed finish.
9. Contact creator info is deliberately public because provided by user, distinct from non-existent developer's sales. Do not solicit visitor data or claim submission to company without working integration.
10. Independent mode state, motion control, time/weather/season, and keyboard/touch 2D path all remain accessible during failure. No splash overlay permanently blocks access.

## V3 deliverable handoff gates
The app must show authored aesthetic quality with actual screenshots, functioning mode switcher/camera paths, localized creator contact and links, measured production build FPS and load metrics on specified device classes, responsive snapshots, real fallbacks, consistent material visual changes, and formal test evidence. No statement 'feature done' without live evidence; failed tests are fixed or transparently marked not shipped. Optional wow features cannot block baseline.



--- FILE: specs/VISUAL_DIRECTION_AND_DESIGN_SYSTEM_V3.md ---
# V3 — PREMIUM DIGITAL ART DIRECTION / DESIGN SYSTEM — MANDATORY

## Product-quality design brief
Create a genuinely exceptional, original, responsive luxury architectural website, not an overdecorated landing template, static Photoshop mockup, PowerPoint, or camera demo with a generic UI slapped over it. Aim for an authored editorial and spatial design that feels appropriate for an international architecture studio yet recognizably Basra-inspired. The website itself must be as art-directed as the tower. The user's satisfaction depends on *micro-level craft*: composition, rhythm, hierarchy, typography, interaction, transitions, trust, comfort, responsiveness, and reliable functionality.

## Hierarchy and storytelling
- A first-visit buyer should understand in 3 seconds: project name, what's being explored, concept status, and the obvious action. The architectural object and user's current context are always dominant; no persistent 35%-wide sidebar obscuring the building.
- Visual hierarchy: (1) building/scene (2) currently selected spatial object (3) primary action (4) brief, factual context (5) optional controls (6) advanced settings under discoverable disclosure.
- Treat page design as immersive editorial architecture: beautiful asymmetrical grid on large screens where appropriate, generous negative space, strict alignment, strong type scale, quiet color. No excessive decorative icons, faux luxury gradients, giant glass panels, parallax overload, or moving text while people read.
- A restrained, original brand lockup based on an abstract palm frond / vertical architectural rhythm; usable at 24px and in monochrome. Ensure vector source is included. Do not claim a trademark registration.
- Scene continuity: consistent camera horizon, tone mapping, white balance, finish choices and labels from exterior to floor to room to gallery.

## Mandatory creative design exploration (Claude autonomously chooses)
Research at least 3 relevant, genuinely different design directions beyond one mood board; document dated sources and asset licenses; prototype one screen and one 3D+UI composition for each; evaluate appearance, readability, brand fit, performance, Arabic typography, one-handed mobile operation, and development time. Pick the best rather than an unexamined first template. Record comparisons and a short rationale in `project_artifacts/DESIGN_DECISIONS.md`. Keep final as *Modern Iraqi Luxury* regardless of chosen interface layout.

## Foundation design tokens
- Color roles, not magic numbers: canvas, elevated surface, panel, primary ink, muted text, subtle separator, bronze accent, focus, success, warning, error, selected, hover, night scheme. Suggested palette is illustrative; audit in context and ensure accessible contrast (WCAG AA for typical text where feasible).
- Typography: licensed Arabic family that renders Arabic marks and numerals beautifully, compatible licensed Latin family, offline/fallback system fonts; defined size, line height, optical spacing, Arabic punctuation, tabular figures, unit area `م² / m²`. Font subsetting/WOFF2 and preload only critical subset. Never load 7 weights.
- Spacing: consistent 4/8px-inspired semantic scale, container max width, dialog/drawer sizes, touch-safe edges, landscape and notch safe areas, 44x44 CSS px minimum interactive targets unless stronger applicable platform rules.
- Shapes: quiet radii; buttons tactile and precise not inflated; crisp 1px dividers; shadows subtle; surface blur sparingly and disable on low-end profiles.
- Elevation, layering, z-index registry: scene, HUD, navigation, drawer, tooltip, modal, safety layer, toast. Modals must not hide primary exit controls; avoid viewport overlap.
- Responsive type: `clamp()` where sensible; Arabic heading line breaks verified; dynamic 200% zoom, device font scaling and browser text reflow. Never use pixel text embedded in renders.

## Component craftsmanship — implement all real states
Header/nav: original wordmark with clear project state, language toggle, quality menu, sound toggle, mode switch, contact creator. Desktop compact top rail, tablet adaptable, phone simplified. Keep explanation labels in onboarding and discoverable tooltips; icons alone should not leave novices guessing.
Buttons: primary, secondary, ghost, icon, destructive/reset with explicit labels; hover, pressed, focus-visible, disabled, in-progress, success, error; animated states 120–220ms and reduced-motion fallback. No button that appears functional but does nothing.
Cards: floor/unit, room, material sample, finish package, image gallery, amenity cards; consistent visual grammar and accessible hit targets; selected state never communicated by color alone.
Controls: precise time slider with keyboard value, season/weather preset picker, camera control and quality selector. Drawer with touch handle, swipe-to-close opt-in, safe area padding, focus trap only for modal dialogs, no body-scroll lock trapping the user. Subtle tactile feedback (visual, not forced vibration).
Material thumbnails: actual texture/finish distinction, not arbitrary stock squares; selected surface outlined; before/after views use **same camera & exposure**. State change is visible and reversible. Never auto-scroll away from the object selected.
Loading: show responsive name/hero/poster before 3D; progressive asset fetch with truthful states (preparing/building/interactive/full quality), optional skip, retry, lite entry. Do not fake percentages or force loading spin after fallback exists.
Toasts: informative but not spammy, one consistent position, accessible nonintrusive announcements. Error messages give concrete recovery actions. Avoid celebratory confetti, fake scarcity counters, intrusive sales prompts.
Details: careful ellipsis, no widows/orphans where practical, variable labels in Arabic/English, elegant empty/loading/error, precise icon alignment, keyboard focus rings, disabled explanations, consistent metric formatting, verified tab order.

## Microinteractions and scene-linked UX
- Hover floor: brief (100–180ms) warm matte accent + floor label, never frantic neon glow; pressed/selected persistence via floor rail and 3D outline. Pointer magnetism should NEVER move controls unexpectedly.
- Scene transitions are camera moves tied to meaningful spatial change, not transitions for decoration. Use easing curves with velocity/jerk review; no abrupt cut when approaching floor/interior; no forced multi-second waits. Provide interrupt / skip / rewind / back.
- Unit cutaway: architectural separation with correct section mask; selected unit gets clean border fill and readable name. Floors not selected should not remain rendered at full interior detail.
- Room entrance: at first enter, frame a compelling hero composition; the user must immediately know where they are, how to return, and how to move. Camera must not enter geometry, clip furniture, swing violently or teleport without a clear choice.
- Materials: pointer hover outlines only approved surfaces, selection results in calm edit panel, swatches visibly update true mesh surface. In-progress texture update shows tiny progress only if needed, never blank scene; preserve earlier choice on asset error.
- Seasons/time/weather: subtle transition to sky/lighting/shadow/foliage and indoor reflections with no disorienting camera reset. Use realistic limits for Basra-inspired concept; fake snow never implied as typical Basra weather. Accessibility option to reduce particle effects.
- Prefer scene-specific tasteful ambient soundtrack OFF by default and always user-controlled, licensed or original. Preserve silence in business settings and respect prefers-reduced-motion.

## Signature screens to actually design (not just describe)
01 Brand Hero / Title Reveal. 02 Tower Overview / property explorer. 03 Selected Floor / Cutaway. 04 Unit Floorplan & Dollhouse. 05 Interior Tour. 06 Material Atelier / Configurator. 07 Gallery & Compare. 08 Wellness / Pool & Gym. 09 Sky Restaurant / Café / Shisha conditional. 10 My Design & Contact. 11 About Experience / Creator Contact. 12 Lite/2D recovery. Also share preview image and printable finish board.

## Artifacts required before final UI implementation
`project_artifacts/design/` should contain: visual-research citations; 3 compared art directions; chosen style guide, logo SVG, token manifest, primary/secondary button states, typography specimens Arabic/English, mobile/desktop/tablet layout comps, interaction/storyboard shots, motion principles with reduced-motion equivalents, dark/light color contrast audit, and actual browser screenshots after implementation. If image generation is unavailable, use original Blender renders and vector design; no fake placeholder image as proof.

## Editorial quality review
Every screen undergoes visual inspection at mobile portrait, mobile landscape, tablet, desktop and ultra-wide: alignment, realistic hierarchy, legibility over moving 3D, touchability, information density, loading/failure state, animation comfort, copy precision and linked experience. Final scorecard is independent of code tests: excellent code with bad visuals is NOT accepted; excellent visuals with non-working buttons is NOT accepted.



--- FILE: specs/EXPLORATION_CAMERA_MODES_V3.md ---
# V3 — MULTI-MODE EXPLORATION + COMFORT-FIRST CAMERA CONTROL

## Four independently usable modes (single coherent scene/state architecture)
A. **Cinematic Tour / الجولة السينمائية**: optional crafted film-style 60–120-second guided narrative with editorial overlay and authored shots: exterior title, architectural flyby, selected floor glow, lift/separate floor, unit cutaway, enter room, material live swap, wellness, pool, sky restaurant, final contact. The experience should be skippable, pauseable, scrub-friendly if viable, muteable, with chapter list, quality-aware alternate shots, precise shot start/end frames, and immediate interruption to explore. It does NOT autoplay upon page load, hijack wheel scrolling, force soundtrack, or keep camera moving after user actively intervenes.
B. **Guided Tour / التجوال الموجّه**: navigable next/previous guided hotspots like professional architectural virtual tour; intuitive camera teleport or short linear glide room-to-room; on-screen room map, directional orientation, and optional explanatory tooltip per room. Works with single finger, mouse and keyboard; suitable for first-time consumers and low-motion users. Avoid excessive fisheye panoramas; no fake rooms represented as constructed spaces.
C. **Free Walk / التجوال الحر**: true bounded first-person movement inside genuinely modeled supported areas: desktop WASD/arrows + mouse-look only AFTER click-to-enter and clear escape; optional controller support; phone touch-look + optional minimal joystick, tap-to-move and hotspot targets; tablet touch equivalent. Collision/navmesh/doors/railings, camera eye-level, stairs/elevator transition as explicit destination, no walk-through-walls, speed limits and gentle acceleration, avoid camera bob, toggle sprint OFF by default, comfort height fixed. Accessible alternative guided hotspot path. Pause movement on modal/drawer; exit with persistent visible control or Esc.
D. **Property Explorer / مستكشف العقار**: reference-reel-style default experience: user orbits exterior, hovers/selects 12 levels, chooses 18 distinct unit IDs with repeated A/B models honestly, sees exploded floor/dollhouse, click-to-enter rooms and amenities, then materials, photo gallery and saved choices. Discrete task-driven transitions. Wheel scroll must remain normal page scroll unless 3D control was directly engaged; no scroll hijacking.
PLUS **Lite 2D Mode**: not treated as an inferior broken fifth mode; universally functional semantic building/floor/unit UI, interactive floor plans, gallery, materials, share/export and contact, with no WebGL dependence. It cannot claim true 3D freewalk. Option always available and automatic on unsuitable hardware/WebGL failure.

## Mode persistence contract
Use ONE centralized validated `ExperienceState` (mode, floorId, unitId, roomId, material overrides, weather, season, time, language, quality, cameraBookmark, playState, 2D fallback). Switching modes MUST NOT erase spatial/material/weather selections. Preserve last user camera per mode and per space; use explicit camera bookmarks. If navigation target incompatible (e.g. switching from F11 café to residential-only material studio), show an understandable transition choice; never silently teleport to unrelated Floor 01. Browser Back and deep links restore appropriate selection. Unavailable mode has clear explanation and nearest usable alternative. On any mode change, cancel prior animation, flush pending camera commands safely and avoid competing control loops.

## Comfort and interruption
- Never trap pointer lock; require user action, Esc exits, visible Exit Walk accessible in mobile and desktop; disable on touch environments where unsupported and offer tap-to-move.
- Camera movement: acceleration and braking continuous, angular velocity capped, no uncontrolled camera roll, spring overshoot or strong zoom. Short smooth transition default; setting for Instant transitions and `prefers-reduced-motion` overrides with cut/no zoom. Keep stable horizon and no shaky motion.
- Drag gestures: one-finger rotates or selects depending mode with clear tutorial, pinch zoom in orbit; two-finger pan optional, no conflicts with browser gestures and page scrolling. Portrait overlays do not consume entire viewport; UI hides/auto-dims gracefully.
- Collision mesh separated from render mesh, door openings validated, navmesh connectors for G / F01–F11, lift stops. True walkable rooms have door connection, reliable spawn/destination anchors and camera clearances. No invented unlimited navigability for unmodeled rooms; show guided still/gallery instead.
- When user activates a UI panel, suspend locomotion and keep camera stable. On close resume only after explicit intent when pointer lock is involved. Inputs not blocked by load; no offscreen canvas interaction ghost focus.
- Allow Reset View, Back to Unit, Jump to Floor, Open Mini Map, Help, Exit Mode (persistent), emergency 2D fallback. Adapt control help to input type.

## Camera implementation detail and quantitative evaluation
Define camera graph nodes, speed/angle limits, path length and room bounding volumes, navmesh walkable polygons, collision obstacles, portal transitions and saved anchors in the production scene manifest. Smooth the velocity rather than arbitrary frame-to-frame lerp; respect delta-time caps and loss-of-focus handling. RequestAnimationFrame/render-on-demand appropriate for active motion, pause expensive work when tab hidden. Use camera raycasting from cursor or explicit hotspot hitboxes, without event handling conflict with drag orbit. Avoid camera passing through opaque walls on transitions: use safe navigable waypoints, temporary accurate cutaway, or a clearly labeled instant scene switch.
Set provisional comfort targets during research; actual acceptance = real measured camera tests with no clipping, no loss of navigation, no perceptible stutter on supported devices and reduced-motion alternate. Do not claim precise Hz/FPS guarantee on untested hardware.

## Spatial continuity and showroom truth
The user should understand *where* they are at all times (floor number, unit number, room, compass/orientation if verified, breadcrumb). Object selection highlight maps to material ID and QA provenance. An interior claimed to be 3D free-walk is actual navigable geometry, not a still or rotating image. Panoramic still alternative clearly labeled. Representative renders must not deceptively reflect unrendered custom finishes.

## Mode-specific acceptance tests
- Start Cinematic -> pause in bedroom -> enter Free Walk -> exit -> resume tour from correct chapter; finish choices remain.
- Property Explorer -> floor F04 -> U04A -> material switch -> Guided Tour -> edit same living room; persisted exact surface.
- Guided Tour on iPhone in portrait -> Rotate device -> use gestures; no viewport loss/no scroll trap.
- Free Walk from doorway: repeated wall collisions, doorway travel, lift F04->F10, touch joystick optional; no clipping or uncontrolled movement.
- 2D Lite mode no-WebGL: select unit, gallery, inspect materials, finish board, creator contact, Arabic/English, deep link; all functional.
- Cinematic on reduced-motion: user-directed short still transitions, subtitles intact and skip operational.



--- FILE: specs/PRODUCT_UI_BEHAVIOR_AND_MICROINTERACTIONS_V3.md ---
# V3 — INTERACTION POLISH, NAVIGATION, USER ERROR RECOVERY

## Universal UX quality principles
Every meaningful visual element should have a role; every icon should be comprehensible; every hover state needs equivalent tap/focus state. Have clearly visible interactive hotspots without visual clutter. Enforce minimum reliable clickable area, debounced click/drag differentiation, undoable decisions, no repeated onboarding after preference saved, no forced signups, no meaningless animations. Every view and state (idle, selected, loading, success, partial error, offline, 2D fallback, empty, disabled) must have designed copy and visual treatment in BOTH languages.

## Recommended information architecture
Primary rail: Explore, Units, Amenities, My Design, About Creator; secondary in a compact scene toolbar: mode, camera, time/weather/seasons, quality, sound, help. On phone use 4 obvious action categories in bottom nav or compact sheet; never expose 14 tiny floating icons. Area selection data controls next to scene, not sprayed on canvas. Offer contextual action prompts once only (first interaction) and concise instructions on tap.

## One-handed phone detail
Support 320px through wide phones, dynamic browser address bars, iOS safe area, Android gestures, tablet split view, full-screen keyboard and landscape; do not make essential functions depend on hover, double-click, right-click, shift or mouse wheel. Panel/drawer can collapse to restore space. Zoom/drag do not interfere with browser back/swipe from screen edges. Tap-to-teleport hotspots sized for fingers and visible on low contrast scenes. Directly verify scroll restoration and device rotation.

## Motion design contract
Define animation families: intro reveal, floor focus, cutaway, room enter, materials, drawer, gallery, environmental transition. Set duration ranges and easing curve presets, automatically reduce duration/distance on mobile low tier and when reduced-motion. Never run decorative continuous loops when idle. Prefer one meaningful animation per major interaction; layered animations must share a timeline and cancel gracefully. Maintain sub-100ms-to-feedback for click/tap where possible and avoid stalled click results. Visible progress states should reflect real asset loading.

## Original decorative effects, with performance lock
Soft shadow/highlight on floor selection, selective depth of field only on pre-rendered cinematic shots or capable GPUs, bronze rim lighting in hero, subtle responsive blurred panel background, atmospheric haze outdoor only (not indoor fog), gentle mask reveal between cutaway and room. Optional camera dust motes ONLY with a tested effect budget and never in material closeups; never sacrifice FPS for shiny effects. Provide clean switch-off.

## Material configurator UX
On selection show material preview, verified or demo-only specification, origin/manufacturer status, applicability, cost difference only if supplied. Display swatches with names not just colors, same-camera before/after comparison, undo history, reset confirmation for destructive bulk changes. Include quick sample export and saved design feedback. If a texture cannot load, keep earlier material, explain failure, permit retry. All weather/time/mode changes preserve materials. If a product image does not match exact selected finish label as representative.

## Environment UX
Time slider 00:00–23:59 (or intentional stepped intervals), dawn/day/golden hour/night presets, season pills Spring/Summer/Autumn/Winter, weather selector Clear/Partly Cloudy/Overcast/Rain/Dust/Wind with plausible environmental implementation and safe indoor effects. Weather changes have independent optional effect intensity control or auto quality scaling, no camera reset; sun-angle precision never claimed without correct geolocation/site orientation. Screenshots and reports mark 'simulated atmosphere'. Use smooth subtle color temperature/exposure interpolation rather than flashing.

## Menus and trust
Make contact options available on landing and final summary; distinguish REAL CREATOR inquiry from FICTIONAL PROPERTY inquiry. Display true creator phone/email with click-to-call/mailto (WhatsApp optional click only, no unsolicited send). Footer legal and source rights clear. Provide accessible About page with creative credits and fictional project notice. No auto-collecting buyer personal data, no misleading 'booked' success without backend. Any actual inquiry backend requires clear consent and basic abuse controls. Configure SEO/OG without using someone else's logo/brand.

## Required visual QA routines
Inspect and screenshot all screen types at 320, 360, 390, 768, 1024, 1366, 1920, and 2560 widths where possible plus landscape variants; Arabic 200% zoom; keyboard focus; buttons truncation; overdraw; safe areas; tooltip positioning; screenreader; reduced motion; tab-switch pause; material loading error. Don't just run page-level tests, click the real controls and examine actual output. Patch until no known P0/P1 failure. Document untested combinations.



--- FILE: specs/SIGNATURE_CREATIVE_EXPERIENCES_V3.md ---
# V3 — EXTRAORDINARY IDEAS WITH MEASURABLE VALUE, NOT A BLOATED WEBSITE

Claude MUST shortlist innovations using: buyer value, architectural truthfulness, wow factor, implementation complexity, device performance, rights/license, privacy, maintainability, and portfolio demonstration value. Implement Tier A after core app and basic performance passes; Tier B only when affordable/low-risk. If optional effects degrade user experience, auto downgrade or feature-gate instead of harming the baseline.

## Signature 1: One Continuous Camera Journey
A composed, plausible transition from boulevard / tower facade -> highlighted floor -> split slab -> dollhouse -> clicked living room -> material surface, preserving spatial orientation. On low-tier use seamless frame-matched still cuts, never blank screens. Include exact camera bookmarks and no wall clipping.

## Signature 2: The Living Building / يوم داخل نخلة
One optional condensed day-in-the-life interactive montage: sunrise at balcony, midday facade shading, sunset indoor materials, evening café, night pool illumination. Change time while following the same camera framing where possible. User controls playback; never force full animation.

## Signature 3: Sun & Comfort Lens
Show simulated sun path and conceptual shading envelope to help users see which balcony gets direct light; overlay orientation disclaimer until actual north and geography verified. Show clear/summer vs golden-hour/winter compare at a chosen viewpoint, without claiming measured comfort or energy rating.

## Signature 4: Live Material Atelier
Color and roughness swatches cause real PBR material changes to registered surfaces with physically plausible lighting; package A/B split reveal and finish-board generation. Full 'my selections' card preserves surface IDs. A realistic gallery photo that doesn't match is honestly 'illustrative'; generate true current-scene screenshot as optional alternative.

## Signature 5: X-Ray Architecture and Accessible Transparency
Click 'See Inside' to reveal 3D floor stacking/cores, trace selected unit to facade and balcony. Reveal real modeled geometry only, never fake building services without verified drawings. Use scalable proxy meshes on mobile. Show section plane and ghosted exterior when possible.

## Signature 6: Floor-to-View Reveal
At each floor, camera moves to balcony viewpoint; show Basra-inspired illustrative skyline, approximate orientation and concept disclaimer. This emotionalizes floor selection without falsely advertising a real view of a real neighborhood.

## Signature 7: Personal Film / Shareable Design Moment
Save a personalized, screenshot-based card of current floor+room+materials+sky preset with embedded concept notice and creator credit. Compact share link restores safe, validated state without uploading buyer information. Generate social preview image with readable Arabic RTL/English titles. Lite alternative exports a branded 2D summary; no cloud renders required.

## Signature 8: Salesroom Theater Mode
'Present to Client' enlarges 3D, hides dev-oriented controls, increases font/click targets, and guides a scripted 3–5 minute live sales narrative. Salesperson can interrupt and answer a client's question then return; use authentic diagrams and known fields only. Option to prefetch limited key scenes on Wi-Fi, without huge first-download cost.

## Signature 9: Quiet Luxury Mode
One-tap minimal HUD, zero sound, zero unnecessary motion, stable orbit/guided navigation, reduced weather particles; beautiful for older users, motion-sensitive visitors and investors using a big screen. Not a lesser visual quality, it is deliberate sophisticated restraint.

## Signature 10: Contextual Intelligence (no expensive AI API)
Contextual prompts drawn from semantic metadata: 'You are on Floor 04. See balcony', 'Change floor finish', 'Compare Standard and Premium'; local app rule engine, no external model API, no personal profiling. Tiny helpful prompts that disappear after use, not a chatbot that blocks the experience.

## Signature 11: Architectural Detail Story
Opt-in callouts describe why the facade uses shading fins, why chosen material has a particular finish, and differences between conceptual specification and actual manufacture. Link product information only if verified. A meaningful educational layer differentiates sales platform from a flashy render.

## Signature 12: Spatial Audio (optional, never default)
Very soft original ambience or licensed street/birds/water sounds with per-scene controls. No autoplay, no surprise sound on navigation, mute as global state, reduced-data switch. Every sound file documented in asset-license ledger.

## Core-value feature selection
Must ship MVP: modes, functioning project explorer, material live edit, environment options, accessible navigation, performant mobile, designer-grade UI, creator credit and honest demo CTA.
Worth adding before portfolio showcase if QA passes: continuous transitions, curated intro, one day-in-life sequence, one cutaway, view reveal, finish export, salesroom mode.
Future/optional: truly accurate solar data, dynamic 3D weather effects on all devices, personalized real-time full-quality render server, VR/WebXR, AI assistant, live inventory CRM, real quotes.

## Acceptance rule
NO feature may claim all possible designs, exact weather, view, price, finish availability, health safety, or building standards as facts. Deliver visually extraordinary *but trustworthy* demo. Implement spectacular features only when they survive the responsiveness and FPS/device gates.

--- FILE: qa/V3_VISUAL_UX_NAVIGATION_RELEASE_GATES.md ---
# V3 VISUAL / UX / CAMERA / RIGHTS QA RELEASE GATES

## Run real tests, not fake reports
QA matrix contains *test definitions only*. Once Claude builds the app, record actual browser/device versions, screen recordings/screenshots, network shaping, GPU tier, test code and results, retest after fixes. `NOT_RUN` != PASS. The package validator tests only fixture consistency, not built 3D nor real app performance. Genuine iOS Safari/iPad Safari verification requires access to those devices, not just responsive Chromium emulation. Do not publish an external company showcase with known P0/P1 failures.

## Baseline acceptance
- A. Branding: logo SVG crisp, original, copyright/credit exact, email and call work, fictional development notice visible; no borrowed design.
- B. Accessibility: Arabic RTL and English LTR cover all views including panels, contacts, forms, warnings and PDF. Keyboard-only navigation reaches everything; screenreader alternative for 3D; visible focus and 44px touch targets; reduced-motion mode avoids forced camera motion.
- C. Performance: first meaningful content and floor picker before 3D; auto quality degradation; stable mid-range phone; no repetitive WebGL context losses; Lite fallback tested; video/gallery never blocks navigation. Use V2 numeric budgets as targets, measure real results.
- D. Four modes: cinematic, guided, freewalk and property explorer all have distinct functional controls, help, start/stop/back, correct transitions and share selection state. Lite full semantic alternative stays accessible.
- E. Visual: inspect actual 3D scene and UI in portrait + tablet + desktop; contrast over variable sky, elegant scene occlusion, no cheap gradient overload, no overlapping controls, material surfaces visibly change, environmental effects are credible, camera feels comfortable.
- F. Trust: 12 floors, 18 demo units, no F10/F11 apartments, no unlicensed media in public site, demo-only info not represented as real estate inventory. No photo falsely claiming selected dynamic material.
- G. Recoverability: missing font/texture/model, offline, browser history, tab suspend, orientation flip, zoom, touch gesture conflict, pointer lock rejection; always regain a useful navigable interface.

## Visual signoff board
Evaluate each screen 1–5 for composition, typographic finesse, originality, interaction clarity, material believability, animation comfort, responsive polish, performance, consistency. A weighted target ≥4 for major categories can inform iteration but does not replace P0/P1 defect gates. Collect before/after annotated screenshots and list outstanding issues in `project_artifacts/VISUAL_QA_REPORT.md`.

## Comfort trial
Ask human volunteer (if available, no mandatory blocking wait) to complete F04→A→Living→change floor→weather→amenity→creator contact on mobile within a few minutes, without tutorial. Observe mis-taps, confusion and nausea; revise. In purely automated environment, run scripted journeys and explicitly disclose human usability was not tested.

## Scope release guidance
Major visual elements and UX are core, but high-fidelity seasons, cinematic music and particles are additive. Ship a fully functioning simplified fallback rather than a stalled hyperrealistic build. Do not silently weaken requested functionalities; list any scope differences and why.
