مراجعة النسخة الثالثة من حزمة نخلة ريزيدنس — 10 نوفمبر 2026
====================================================
الحزمة الحالية **مواصفات تنفيذ شاملة** وليست موقعاً جاهزاً. تم فحص الملفات والبنية البرمجية لبياناتها، ولم تُختبر مخرجات موقع لم يتم إنشاؤه بعد. لا يوجد ضمان منطقي بـ«ولا غلطة» على جميع الأجهزة؛ بدلاً عنه اعتمدنا متطلبات QA واضحة ودليل إثبات قبل التسليم.

التحديثات:
1- تمت إضافة عقد جماليات كامل للهوية، UX/UI، كل حالة من حالات الأزرار، الخطوط، ألوان الوصول، التفاصيل الدقيقة، التحميل والتعافي، وحركة الكاميرات.
2- أربعة أوضاع: جولة سينمائية يمكن إيقافها، جولة موجهة سهلة، تجوال حر فعلي ضمن مجسمات مبنية، استكشاف عقاري مثل الريل. وLite/2D عند العجز أو ضعف الأجهزة.
3- سلاسة الحركة أولوية على الإفكتات، مع تخطي الانتقالات وتقليل الحركة وإلغاء المسارات القديمة ومنع اختراق الجدران.
4- معلومات منشئ المشروع بالنص الصحيح: Rashid Omar Mohammed / +9647700864990 / rashid.iq12@gmail.com، مع روابط اتصال وصفحة About وفوتر وPDF ووسم أن المشروع تخيلي.
5- أفكار إضافية: يوم سينمائي كامل، محاكاة الظلال، منظور من شرفة كل طابق، Material Atelier، عارض X-Ray، تصميم خاص قابل للمشاركة، Presentation Mode، وQuiet Luxury.
6- حماية من أخطاء قديمة: G+F01..F09+F10+F11 = 12 طابق، 18 شقة؛ معالجة تعارض اللوبي Double-height مع الطابق الأول؛ مسبح علوي مفهوم مشروط بدراسة مختص؛ صور مواد لا تُدّعى مطابقتها إذا لم تكن كذلك.
7- تتبّع اختبارات UI/UX والكاميرا والعلامة والحقوق وبيانات التفاعل، كلها معلمة NOT_RUN، وتُنفذ من قِبل Claude في بيئة التطوير.
8- أي أداة/skill من GitHub يلزم مراجعة الصلاحيات والملفات والرخص والإصدارات وتثبيت محلي من دون دفع غير موافق عليه.
9- الحفاظ على تصاميم Modern Iraqi Luxury ومواصفات الأداء وتجربة الفصول والجو والتجوال من V2 دون إسقاط المتطلبات.

الملف المفترض إرساله: ZIP النهائي V3 فقط. افتح Claude Code بمجلد فارغ، فك الضغط أو أعطِ أداة قادرة على فك الضغط، واطلب منه قراءة START_HERE.txt وتنفيذ المشروع. لا تجمع نفس الوقت MASTER_PROMPT_COMPLETE.txt وكل مجلد specs لأن المحتوى مكرر.


V3 RELEASE AUDIT CORRECTION — CANONICAL SINGLE CAMERA MODE
The old V2 userState.navigationMode is deprecated and MUST NOT be used alongside the new V3 explorationMode. New JSON seed removes navigationMode. explorationMode is the ONLY mode controller state, with values cinematic/guided/free_walk/property_explorer. Migrate old navigationMode gracefully, discard old field. viewMode is a scene layout only, never controller authority. Do not run two camera systems simultaneously. Other V3 requirements prevail.


--- V4 HIGH PRIORITY / FOUR LANGUAGES + SILENT AUDIO CONTRACT ---

# V4 HIGHEST PRIORITY: QUIET BY DEFAULT + FOUR FULLY LOCALIZED LANGUAGES

This V4 specification OVERRIDES any earlier bilingual, locale or sound defaults anywhere in V1–V3. Required locales: **Arabic ar-IQ DEFAULT RTL**, **English en LTR**, **Central Kurdish Sorani ckb-IQ RTL**, **Simplified Chinese zh-Hans LTR**. Do not silently replace Sorani with Kurmanji or Chinese Simplified with Traditional. No language should be a partial demo of an otherwise fully functional website; navigation, 3D hotspot labels, tooltips, amenities, material specifications, status notices, confirmation/error/loading states, footer, creator contact, gallery metadata, PDF exports, tour captions, and accessibility text must all be localized. Preserve IDs, camera and finish selection when switching.

EVERY PAGE LOAD or document reload MUST START WITH **ALL SOUND DISABLED/MUTED**. No autoplay, no jingles, no footsteps, no sky/ambient noises, no narration, no video audio and no Web Audio playing or fetching heavy audio assets. Unmute only from an explicit user-initiated audio control, never inferred from 'Start Tour' alone. Users can mute/disable audio instantly and at any point, including cinematic, free-walk and the welcome screen. Changing page, floor, room, weather, language, scene or tour mode MUST NOT unmute; a new page load MUST revert to muted even if audio was opted into during the previous visit. Sound is optional decoration and must not gate or slow navigation.

Localization quality is a SHIP BLOCKER: no missing keys, machine translation presented as verified, mistranslated safety notices, incorrect direction, broken Arabic/Sorani glyphs, untested PDF/JSON interpolation or misrepresented demo-status translations. Claude must research official language/typographic standards, develop canonical terminology, perform automated checks and arrange/record competent native-speaker review for all four locales before declaring production-grade translation accuracy. If native validation is unavailable, mark those locales **UNVERIFIED / REVIEW NEEDED**, provide working draft UI for demos, and DO NOT claim zero translation errors or final linguistic sign-off. Do not delay technical work waiting for approvals; continue implementation and report gate truthfully.

Required reads before implementation: `specs/V4_AUDIO_QUIET_BY_DEFAULT.md`, `specs/V4_FOUR_LANGUAGE_LOCALIZATION_AND_TRANSLATION_QA.md`, `qa/V4_AUDIO_I18N_RELEASE_GATES.md`, and new `data/locales_manifest.seed.json`, `data/audio_preferences.seed.json`, `data/translation_review_status.seed.json`. Run `python scripts/VALIDATE_V4_PACKAGE.py` in addition to earlier offline checks. These validate PACKAGE FIXTURES ONLY, not the built website. In case of conflict, V4 > V3 > V2 > V1. Do not send or publish the attached Instagram reel or screenshots. Do not auto-deploy a public website.



--- FILE: specs/V4_AUDIO_QUIET_BY_DEFAULT.md ---

# V4 — AUDIO, SILENCE, CONSENT AND RESPECTFUL SOUND DESIGN (MANDATORY)

## 1. Product promise
This experience is SILENT on every first load and every new page load, including return visits. Do not infer permission to play audio from general navigation clicks, selecting a property, selecting language, pressing Start Tour or loading a video. Explicitly turning on an audio control is the ONLY activation. Sound is never required to understand the building and MUST NOT consume noticeable network/CPU/GPU resources while muted.

## 2. One authoritative state machine
- `audio.masterEnabled` default FALSE on every full navigation/reload/new tab. `audio.userOptedInThisPageLoad` false until clear direct sound-control action. In-app SPA routes and changing floor/room/mode do not reset selected on/off state unless the user mutes it; full reload always starts OFF. Never auto-enable from saved storage or browser preference. Existing V2 `ambientSoundEnabled` is deprecated and MUST be removed/migrated to new audio controller.
- `audio.muted` is the only global master mute authority; avoid multiple racing React states or per-scene auto-start scripts. Initial `masterEnabled=false` and `muted=true`. Unmuted user gesture sets masterEnabled=true, muted=false. Re-mute synchronously sets muted=true and stops/suspends ALL output, including background HTMLMediaElements and WebAudio buffers.
- Explicitly opt into selected channels: `music`, `ambience`, `effects`, `narration`, all set to false until user enables master. Once audio is enabled, recommend restrained channel defaults (ambience 0.20, effects 0.28, music 0.12, narration 0.50), with visible independent sliders 0..100. No channel at max by default. Never play loud click/beep on enabling.
- On `visibilitychange` hidden, audio pauses/suspends and does not surprise user when returning; resuming sound requires an explicit user action after a hidden/background interruption. If media play promise rejects (NotAllowedError, etc.), remain muted and non-crashing, show nonmodal localized explanation.
- Strict on/off controls persist in one place: header accessible sound icon on all screen sizes, small control on cinematic overlay, Settings > Sound. Muted icon and text 'Sound Off'/'الصوت مطفأ' etc; do not use color alone. Accessible state `aria-pressed`, localized labels/tooltip, keyboard Space/Enter. 44px tap size, clear focus. Quick sound mute action must work during frame hitch, tour pause, loading screens, fullscreen, WebGL context-loss and Lite fallback.

## 3. Sound aesthetics
- If approved, use an original, unobtrusive audio palette: subtle UI feedback, gentle footsteps only in free walk, tasteful daylight/night ambience, optional guided tour narrative captions. NO looping 10-second royalty-free cliché; NO mandatory intro music; no abrupt stingers at camera transitions; no distracting door creaks/echoes, no surprise thunder or whistling. If ambient SFX tied to season/weather use asynchronous fades (150–400ms) and rate limit; weather change never overrides global mute.
- No geolocation/microphone permissions required. Audio tour is not a listening test. Narration has captions/transcript in FOUR languages, including accessible captions for videos, independent of audio state and optional language-aware voice only if licensed and high quality. Bad or absent voice > omit narration, keep written captions. Never pronounce place names or material safety data with guessed phonemes and claim perfect quality.
- Do not play audio assets remotely on default load: split chunks by tour/scene, wait for opt-in then fetch, cap cache, short loop seamless, compressed lightweight formats with fallback; full silence must be possible even if code for channels is loaded. Do not use music from copyrighted movies/commercial recordings without license. Audio ledger records source, commercial rights, duration and loop status.
- Minimize energy and battery cost: stop WebAudio scheduling/3D positional audio updates when muted or hidden; remove stale sources/listeners; app disposal must dispose AudioContexts and release streams on unmount.

## 4. Edge cases
- VoiceOver/TalkBack accessibility speech is system-controlled: master mute silences only WEBSITE audio, never OS/screen-reader narration. Focus and feedback must work without site audio.
- Video preview may autoplay if permanently muted and user-requested, but never with sound; `playsInline`, no unexpected full-screen switch. Cinematic Tour with sound off MUST remain fully legible through subtitles and visible chapter names.
- Tour controls (pause/restart/skip), language/quality/mode changes, browser history, media interruptions and screenshot export MUST NOT restart sound.
- Treat microphone use, push notifications, external speech APIs and voice assistants as separate opt-in product capabilities; default NONE.

## 5. Validation
Unit tests for initial mute and opt-in state, component tests for mute from EVERY view, browser tests Chrome/Firefox/Safari/iOS emulation and real iOS if available; automation should intercept `play`, `AudioContext.resume`, `fetch *.mp3/*.ogg`, speakers. Starting load without explicit audio opt-in must produce ZERO audible website output and ideally ZERO audio asset transfers. Browser gesture restrictions must be handled as failure states. Console errors or unhandled promise rejections prohibited. Audio button remains reachable in Lite mode.


--- FILE: specs/V4_FOUR_LANGUAGE_LOCALIZATION_AND_TRANSLATION_QA.md ---

# V4 — COMPLETE 4-LANGUAGE SYSTEM AND TRANSLATION RELIABILITY

## 1. Locales and non-negotiable behavior
The primary source copy and first-visit URL/page is Iraqi-friendly professional **Arabic (ar-IQ, RTL)**, NOT an English-first dashboard. Supported secondary locales are **English (en, LTR)**, **Central Kurdish/Sorani (ckb-IQ, RTL)** using Arabic-derived Kurdish script used by many in Iraq's Kurdistan region, and **Simplified Chinese (zh-Hans, LTR)** suitable for Chinese-speaking visitors. Kurmanji (ku-Latn) is a DIFFERENT variety and not a substitution. If ever adding Kurmanji in the future, make it a separate language, script and review flow. Use locale-aware display names in native script: العربية | English | کوردی (سۆرانی) | 简体中文.

Arabic must have good Iraqi audience tone and engineering terminology (modern professional Arabic rather than casual machine slang); preserve marketing refinement. Chinese must be locally natural, not literal character-by-character translation. Kurdish Sorani must be written by someone who knows that dialect's script (ڕ, ڵ, ۆ, ێ, ە, etc.), correct keyboard/typography, NOT borrowed Persian or Arabic words where natural Kurdish technical terms exist. Keep English technology terms where customary and properly directional-isolated. Brand NAKHLA RESIDENCE is a brand; translations/localized script representations must be intentional and approved, never inconsistent across headers, PDFs and logos.

## 2. Scope inventory—every visible string (NO exceptions)
Hero, navigation, switcher, icons tooltips and aria labels, mode descriptions, progress bars, onboarding and skip, splash/loading/queued/failed states, model fallback, 3D hotspot text, floor/unit/room labels, descriptions, dimensions, material and package glossary, supplier placeholders, demo and data disclaimers, SEO/OG/meta, floor availability, Material Studio, price warnings, currency/units, weather and all four seasons, audio channels and volume labels, free walk help and collision errors, X-Ray/isolated floor, screenshots/photo licensing labels, gallery proof/selection mismatch notices, local save/share/QR, failed upload/save events, contact CTA, creator rights/copyright, privacy and form copy, tooltips/hints, access keys, error boundaries, accessibility live regions, keyboard shortcuts and PDF export; do not hard-code untranslated English in 3D canvas/GSAP timelines. Structured material descriptions and any future backend content require content localization too, not just 35 seed strings.

## 3. Architecture contract
- Single shared localization service; structured ICU MessageFormat or equivalent evaluated, versioned dictionary. Never string-concatenate translated sentences around variables or mix arbitrary `innerHTML` and translations. Typed localization keys generated/enforced; `Intl` for number, currency, dates and units; sanitize interpolated values; pluralization for rooms, floors, photos, guests, etc. Handle zero/one/two/plural differences correctly, especially Arabic.
- Use HTML `<html lang="ar-IQ" dir="rtl">` initially; on switch set accurate `lang` and `dir` at document root and main content, update tab title, metadata, accessible labels, page route, and PDF locale; use semantic `dir="auto"` or bdi/isolates for English technical terms, phone numbers, URLs and product codes embedded in RTL. LTR diagrams/camera coordinate overlays stay physically consistent; directional icon arrows and breadcrumbs should mirror logically, NEVER flip the 3D model or viewer camera itself. JSON IDs and room IDs stay fixed.
- Suggested locale route prefix `/ar/`, `/en/`, `/ckb/`, `/zh/`; resolved locale mapping to ar-IQ/en/ckb-IQ/zh-Hans; first-load default `/ar/` unless visitor manually selects another. Preserve selected locale through in-app navigation and subsequent visits if desired, but NEVER override an explicit URL locale. Detect unsupported locale -> default Arabic, not 404. Remember selected locale separate from audio preferences. Switching language while standing in F04/U04A/living keeps apartment, selected material, lighting/weather, current tour progress bookmark, camera pose and mute state; do not reinitialize Three.js or WebGL context. Language may influence content layout but not semantic state.
- Use accessible, bundled/subset fonts with complete Arabic/Sorani glyph support and Simplified Chinese glyph support. Verify weight, shaping, line height, hyphenation, punctuation, emoji fallback, button width and focus rings in Safari/Chrome/Firefox Android/iOS. Use reliable font licenses and avoid huge global font packs; language-scoped subsets lazy loaded after first paint, fallback fonts without FOIT; avoid >3MB first-load CJK font payload by subsetting/appropriate fallback.
- Provide robust RTL grid/flex layouts with logical CSS (`padding-inline-start`, etc.), no clipped sidebars, no direction changes that break 3D scene or drag direction. Handle responsive UI and text expansion (English vs Chinese vs long Arabic / Sorani) at 320, 360, 390, 768, 1024 and 1440 widths with 200% browser zoom and dynamic text size. Touch targets 44px and clear hit areas. Independent scroll in panels must not conflict with 3D touch gestures.
- Locale-aware unit formatting for `m²`, `cm`, °C, time of day, level numbers, and dates. Never numerically alter measured areas or quote fabricated architectural precision. Latin email/tel/WhatsApp markup stays valid in all locales and correctly isolated.

## 4. Translation editorial workflow (do NOT hallucinate perfection)
A. Inventory all message keys in static UI, dynamically generated text and server data; make ALL four locale dictionaries key-complete. Mark legacy strings needing rewriting. Define canonical Arabic termbase with approved English, Sorani and Chinese equivalents for building, floor, unit, room, balcony, material, finishes, waterproofing, lighting, gym, pool, sauna, restaurant, hookah/shisha lounge, demo project, copyright, creator, unavailable and request information. Preserve distinctions between residential floor, functional floor and building floor number (G, F01..F11).
B. First draft can be by Claude/MT but must carry explicit provenance and `reviewStatus='DRAFT_UNVERIFIED'`. Native language editors/reviewers with domain context must validate EACH string, then independently check HIGH-RISK statements (legal/privacy, availability, accessibility instructions, measurements, cash/price, environment claims, health/safety, shisha rules). If reviewers are not accessible, produce exact review-ready bilingual term sheets, `REVIEW_REQUIRED` flag in QA, and do not pretend validation happened. Do not post sensitive project files to 3rd-party translation APIs without authorization.
C. Review in contextual screenshots, not just a spreadsheet; check localization in actual 3D floor/unit detail, materials, 4 camera modes, audio control and PDF output. Back translation and pairwise comparisons are optional supportive heuristics but not substitutes for native review.
D. CI must fail on missing/extra/unreachable keys, falsy placeholders, untranslated UI English leftovers (where not intentional brand/product), HTML escape or ICU syntax errors, wrong tokens, truncation/ellipsis of important terms, plural and gender violations, mismatched glossary entries, key collisions, duplicate locale paths, untranslated chart labels or SEO metadata. Integration tests verify state survives locale change, tab order, locale URL, rtl/ltr flips, no changed floor id or material mapping, and no accidental sound unmute.
E. Do not mark a locale `APPROVED` before documented reviewer name/role, review date, text/catalog version hash, screenshot proof, and second-person review for legal/safety strings. If unapproved by release, disclose clearly to owner; a private concept demo can include draft locales with accurate caveat, but do not label production-ready.

## 5. User-centered enhancements
- Language menu uses native labels and abbreviated locale codes; always visible in header, in fullscreen Explorer via Info/Settings, accessible with keyboard/screenreader. No flags as language icons (flags represent countries, not languages). Preview translated tour chapters and audio mute toggle before applying.
- Language transition should be instantaneous and should not create reload, recompile 3D models or restart cinematic tour; no moving camera during a language change. Persist chosen language for later pages but don't force it on explicit locale URL.
- In 2D Lite mode all interactive explanatory text is equally localized, so lower-spec visitors are not second-class. Localize skeleton and retry descriptions; no strange untranslated technical errors shown directly to customers.
- For public pages add `hreflang` alternates if indexing is enabled, canonical URL policy and localized sitemap/metadata; don't duplicate a real building claim in SEO.

## 6. Non-negotiable verification
4-language screenshot sweep at mobile/tablet/desktop in light/dark or key sky themes; screen-reader auditing for ar and ckb RTL; PDF documents checked visually with valid embedded fonts and not broken Unicode; numeric formatting test at every locale and in mixed-script strings; CJK font-load weight and cache tests; voice/media mute remains off during locale switch. A build with untranslated strings is not ready. If native reviewer unavailable: cannot claim 'mistake free translations' regardless of automated test count.

# V4 RELEASE GATES — SOUND + 4 LOCALES

Do not equate fixture validator PASS with running website PASS. Update actual QA rows only after testing with evidence. Blocking P0:
1. All new page loads are SILENT. No website sound without direct deliberate unmute click; browser autoplay restrictions handled correctly. Return visit/reload always silent.
2. Sound toggle accessible on hero, all four exploration modes, fullscreen, Material Studio and Lite; hard mute stops ALL channels and doesn't re-enable on floor, room, weather, language, performance tier, reconnect or camera transition.
3. Arabic ar-IQ is default, layout RTL. en LTR, ckb-IQ RTL, zh-Hans LTR. Root lang/dir and metadata update on locale choice. No broken glyphs or untranslated placeholders on key routes.
4. Locale switch during a tour preserves room, floor, unit, material selection, environment, camera mode/bookmark and sound state (especially mute); avoid WebGL scene recreation. Navigation remains functional.
5. 4-locale completeness: dynamic texts, material information, amenity text, photo captions, creator contact, legal/demo notices, SEO and exported PDF. No fake translations; signed linguistic review or explicit unverified flag.
6. Mixed-script telephone/email technical IDs in RTL are read and clickable correctly. Sorani script shaping, Chinese font load, large fonts, 320px width, browser zoom 200%, tablets, safe areas and iOS landscape pass.
7. Auditable native/editor translation acceptance before final production-grade linguistic claim; if missing: show gap in delivery report instead of claiming zero errors. Include no new paid translation/voice services without permission.

Recording: screenshots for four locales × hero/floor/interior/settings/contact; browser audio console instrumentation; manual test on real iOS and Android where accessible; PDF comparison; exact status NOT_RUN/PASS/FAIL/BLOCKED/REVIEW_NEEDED and evidence path. Avoid untestable universal device claims.


PRIMARY AUDIO STATE FIX: V4 userState.audio replaces V2 ambientSoundEnabled. PRIMARY LOCALE FIX: ar/en/ckb/zh with Arabic default. Never claim native-reviewed translations without signoff.

Extra 'Sun & View', 'Architect', 'Furnishing', 'Presentation', 'Smart Finder', and 'AI' are feature tools/panels, NOT globally visible modes. specs/V5_INFORMATION_ARCHITECTURE_AND_REVEAL.md

# V5 — PRODUCT INFORMATION ARCHITECTURE: MAKE POWER FEEL SIMPLE

## Product principle
A first-time Iraqi buyer, older adult, investor, salesperson and foreign visitor must all understand what to do within seconds. Aim for "one primary next action at a time". Visual richness should sit in the scene, not the control density. Do not invent sections for every feature; instead use exactly four user-facing top-level sections, a gentle introductory hero, one persistent summary action, and contextual overlays. Arabic first, same level of product completeness in English, Sorani Kurdish and Simplified Chinese.

## Four user-facing sections (fixed)
### SECTION 1: استكشف | Explore
Role: location/building/floor/unit orientation and decision. Starts at immediate interactive hero; never mandatory intro or popup. Show 12-level tower, selectable floor bands, accessible floor list, selected floor label, simple two-unit cards for residential floors, and truthful amenity zone labels on F10/F11. "Find my apartment" opens a SHORT drawer/wizard; show up to 3 matches first. "Compare" appears only after a unit is selected or marked. "Sun & View" appears beside the selected floor/unit/balcony, not a global mode. Optional X-Ray in More tools. The same IDs and geometry across exterior, section view, plan and rendered room. Floor F10 wellness and F11 dining NEVER contain apartments. No invented availability or sales price.

### SECTION 2: تجوّل | Tour
Role: experience the selected apartment/room and move without discomfort. Starting default is a safe stable room camera; tapping hotspots or room chips transports user predictably. One simple control 'طريقة الجولة / Tour style' opens FOUR original supported camera modes (Cinematic, Guided, Free Walk, Property Explorer) with concise 1-line descriptions and supported device info. This is a subordinate selector, NOT four extra permanent tabs. Change modes without teleport surprise, invalid camera anchor, renderer reinit or losing selection. Cinematic is opt-in; cancel/skip/pause; no autoplay sound. Free Walk uses collision, capped acceleration, no roll, gentle turn, stop/restore, safe floor/room transitions, touch tap-to-move alternative and reset orientation. 'A Day in Nakhla' is ONE optional story tile/chapter carousel inside Tour; never forced. Room-specific 'Measure/Furnish' tool only if coordinate accuracy confirmed; otherwise clearly conceptual visual fit.

### SECTION 3: صمّم | Design
Role: understand and customize REAL material bindings on the current scene. Step 1 choose area (floor/wall/cabinet/etc) by object click or accessible list; step 2 view actual material details and 2–4 relevant choices; step 3 apply with reversible undo and instant visible 3D update; step 4 compare before/after and save. Put AI-style suggestions under 'اقتراح تنسيق / Style suggestions', deterministic on-device presets first and never imply generative AI ran without API. No uncontrolled 'design anything' button or impossible images. Share and PDF summary in one consistent 'شقتي / My Apartment' drawer, not another tab. All gallery images explicitly explain whether they correspond to current selections.

### SECTION 4: المرافق | Amenities
Role: discover F10 (pool, gym, spa/sauna) and F11 (café, restaurant, outdoor optional separate shisha and panoramic terrace). Have concise illustrated tiles and 'Visit in 3D' only for modeled scenes; 2D high-fidelity alternatives otherwise. A contextual quick route to the amenity floor. Safety/engineering caveats for elevated pool; no shisha regulatory claims. 'See day-in-life' link can invoke curated chapters on those amenity scenes.

## Persistent chrome (small, consistent)
- Elegant brand + main four-tab navigation desktop; on mobile bottom four-icon/label navigation with safe-area spacing and clear icon captions, not 8-item hamburger.
- Breadcrumb 'Building > F04 > Unit A > Living' with Back and Home; state invalidation guarded for F10/F11.
- A small 'My Apartment' summary/bookmark with count and CTA, plus ability to visit About Creator/Contact.
- Single compact utility menu for Language, Sound (OFF until explicit user click, reset on EACH hard page load), Help, Accessibility/Reduce Motion, Quality Lite, Credits and About.
- A compact environment/time control is shown only on selected viewpoints or scene contexts; advanced weather/season presets in its own collapsible panel; never float five panels on mobile.
- Only one active side panel; Escape/back key closes it before navigating away. Mobile bottom sheet and safe-area. Dismissible overlays never cover critical navigation or trap focus.

## Reveal rules and state
- Start without login wall, forced video, autoplay or modal; first meaningful render shows architectural preview and actionable Explore even before 3D loads.
- At first visit, one tiny nonblocking 2-step hint max; Help accessible thereafter, never repeated intrusive tours.
- Progressive disclosure: primary action (one); secondary affordances max 2; advanced actions via 'More'. Hide unsupported or inaccurate capabilities, show clear explanation instead of disabled dead buttons.
- Persistence across section switching: locale, selected floor/unit/room, camera bookmark where compatible, material overrides, environment and choices. Preserve sound mute. When switching to F10/F11, retain 'last residential unit' separately without assigning apartments to amenities. Do not erase selections on Section back.
- Navigation can be interrupted; tap of new floor cancels old camera tween and stale loading request. No unresponsive scroll, scroll-jacking or forced animation waiting.
- Touch targets >=44 CSS px ideally (minimum hitboxes), accessible focus, text zoom, RTL-aware order and icon direction, dynamic type, screen-reader equivalents, reduced motion and 2D alternative parity.
- Four tabs labeled in ALL FOUR locale dictionaries, not flag emoji. No silent untranslated copy on new panels.
- Preserve human choice: environment sim, cinematic, presentation, AI tools are opt-in and reversible.

## Example zero-confusion flow (buyer)
1. Open link; see name, clear visual and 'استكشف'.
2. Tap floor 04 -> Unit A. See approximate area, bedrooms and 'ادخل للشقة'.
3. Go to Tour and tap Living; stable camera, obvious room map/back.
4. Go Design; tap floor, compare two samples, save.
5. Open My Apartment, see specs/render mismatch caveat, Share / Export / Contact Creator.
6. Can visit Amenities and back; selections retained.

## Visual quality constraints
Premium Modern Iraqi Luxury = refined warm ivory, bronze, charcoal; generous whitespace, fewer heavier clear typography, bespoke original architectural assets, consistent microcopy, one tasteful 200–400 ms transition where measured comfortable, no 8-layer parallax. Avoid excessive heavy DOM blur, infinite scroll effects, flashing/glowing floor labels, unreadable tiny secondary text. High performance under modest Iraqi network beats theatrical intro.


========================================================================
specs/V5_SMART_PROPERTY_AND_DAYLIGHT.md
========================================================================
# V5 — FIND THE RIGHT APARTMENT, NOT JUST A NICE PICTURE

## Smart Apartment Finder (P1, rule-based, offline demo)
Open from Explore as contextual drawer. Show optional minimal controls: bedrooms (2/3), level preference (low/mid/high/any), target area band (concept approx), sun aspect/view preference (only known/illustrative orientation), and quiet-vs-amenity proximity (only geometry-grounded). Max 2 questions at a time; optional skip; no signup. Avoid asking incomes, protected traits, real names, health or other sensitive attributes. For current concept: 9 residential floors with Type A approx 125m² and Type B approx 145m². Deterministic, explainable ranking; label 'Match suggestion, not verified availability'. Weights are configurable and visible to developer; do not fabricate availability, direction, actual pricing or exact views. Output up to 3 cards, each with 'Why this match', a Select in 3D button and Compare checkbox. Empty state offers useful reset.

## Two-Apartment Compare (P1)
Only two units at a time. Stable comparison fields: floor, unit type, approximate area, bedroom/bath count, concept balcony, finish package, selected environmental preview, illustrative daylight and view. Highlight actual differences, not decorative checkmarks. Never compare fictional prices to imply real sale. Works fully in 2D Lite. Horizontal split cards on desktop; stacked scroll + sticky labels on mobile; RTL and Chinese overflow tested. Persist unit A as active until user explicitly selects another unit. Accessible toggle to close comparison.

## Sun & View Lens (P1 illustrative; accurate research-grade calculations P2)
- Daylight slider + season selector attach to current apartment view and shadow-casting scene. Compute sun direction from date/time and **verified** place coordinates/north orientation only when actually known; otherwise mark 'illustrative sunlight simulation'. No claim exact hours of sunlight, thermal comfort, cooling loads or UV rating.
- Floor-to-View reveal: choose balcony/window, display elevation-dependent camera framing, Basra-inspired sky and urban context. Unless verified survey/panorama and license available, prominently label 'Conceptual view — not a real view from a property'. Never imply guaranteed river view.
- Weather: existing seasonal controls should adapt sky, ambient light and glass appropriately, not heavy rain effects in indoor pool or on all low-power devices. Sun shading compare may toggle between morning/evening and summer/winter in a compact sheet.
- Provide reset, pause, skip and 2D static comparison even without WebGL.
- Sun path coordinates must be in ONE canonical right-handed world system aligned to known north and floor geometry; tests catch mirrored RTL changes (RTL must NEVER mirror 3D north).

## Architectural X-Ray Pro (P2 optional)
Must show real modeled layer/cutaway/section, no phantom plumbing or MEP; preserve camera/selection when toggling. On phone show elegant selective opacity/static cutaway if translucent 3D costs too much. Show low/high detail details through progressive load. An Architect Tools contextual action, not global navigation. If engineering input absent, label illustrative building anatomy not BIM.

## Zero-trust factual copy
All factual metrics from approved source-backed unit catalog only. If no exact site, view or solar study, use concept labels in all locales, share/export and screenshots. Materials and measurements should never be interpreted as purchasing specs until verified.

# V5 — DESIGN STUDIO: PHYSICAL FEEDBACK + HONEST PERSONALIZATION

## Material config remains canonical (P0)
Keep six material categories and 18 options from V4, semantic surface IDs + real PBR updates in visible modeled rooms, inspect/edit/undo/reset, finish package consistency, and representative gallery warning. New V5 must not fork this state or create a second material store. Image gallery is a different fidelity tier; only label 'exact' when a generated frame genuinely matches the chosen variant.

## Style Suggestions / AI Interior Designer (P2 stageable)
Default zero-cost local recommendation engine uses tested constraints and designer-authored presets (Warm Iraqi Luxury, Modern Pearl, Calm Minimal, etc). User enters or chooses a preference; parser supports 4 languages without free-text unsafe exec. Suggestions map only to AVAILABLE catalog materialIds/furnitureIds. The user PREVIEWS and confirms changes; track diffs and undo. Never pretend prompts can generate arbitrary photoreal 3D changes instantly. A true external AI assistant is behind a disabled-by-default feature flag and must not be enabled before approved cost/privacy/security review. Explain the difference visibly in product copy if marketed.

## Furniture Fit + Measurements (P2)
Use measured and verified Blender-authored room meshes with consistent meter scale, door arcs, accessible paths and safe bounding boxes. 2D dimension tape and measurement anchors exported from source geometry; camera-based visual approximation must be marked approximate. Furniture placement: catalogue of 3-5 original/licensed models e.g. sofa, king bed, dining set, rug, table; snap-to-valid floor, rotation, collision, no clipping through wall, keep egress route unobstructed in demo (do not imply code certification). Undo and remove; collision not merely drawing label. Mobile simple variants or furniture swatch placement with no drag needed. Preserve selections across scene unload and export under namespaced state. Do not display fictional store SKU/brand/stock.

## Material/Apartment Compare (P1)
Simple compare starts contextual from unit card or from Finish Studio. For finishes show before/after slider or A/B swatches with synchronized camera, lighting and geometry. No simultaneous full WebGL renderers on low-tier mobile; reuse single scene + snapshot or split stills. Comparing 2 apartments requires two different unit IDs while retaining active unit state (see Smart Property spec). Accessible text table equivalent is mandatory.

## Save/Share/My Apartment (P1)
One accessible 'My Apartment' bottom sheet shows current floor, chosen unit, finish package overrides, gallery consistency notice and conceptual property warning. Local-only default no account. Share state link by compact validated versioned schema: allowlisted catalog IDs, no names, phone, email, raw scripts, tracking or private visitor data; impose strict size limit and degrade when not enough URL budget. QR code optional generated locally from sanitized current URL (nothing uploaded). Do not claim 'saved on server' without backend. Export image or print-to-PDF with working RTL/CKB/CJK glyphs, creator attribution and concept notice. Broken/share unsupported fallback is one-click copy of textual summary and visible explanation.

## Extras
Bookmark camera/view/environment as a single scene view state, not multiple confusing 'save' buttons. Optional 'share design postcard' is generated locally where browser canvas permissions allow; if tainted canvas or GPU unavailable, generate HTML-to-image/2D summary. No visitor input sent to API silently. Never reuse private raw Instagram frames in public postcards.

# V5 — SALESROOM PRESENTATION, STORYTELLING AND A REUSABLE BUSINESS PRODUCT

## Day-in-Your-Future-Home (P1)
A deliberate user-activated 'يوم في نخلة' interactive short story inside Tour, with 5 skippable chapters: (1) sunrise balcony, (2) living and material detail, (3) afternoon wellness/gym/pool, (4) sunset dining/cafe, (5) night exterior. Cinematic camera, optional captions in all 4 languages, NO sound without explicit opt-in, reduced-motion chapter stills and Lite HTML carousel. Every chapter is an authentic prepared scene/camera anchor; cancellation at any point restores last user room/finishes/time unless user chooses 'Apply Scene Settings'. Keep one renderer active; preload one next chapter at a time, not all assets in initial transfer. Target a concise ~60–100s optional experience, timing flexible. Do not imply pool/spa/shisha legally approved or real construction exists.

## Presentation Room (P1)
An opt-in salesperson-friendly presentation action under utility/More (not a fifth section): large 3D, big readable labels, minimal panel, sequence 3–5 minutes focusing on buyer value. Prev/Next/Jump/Exit, keyboard and tablet control, capture/resume current viewer state, audience-friendly captions 4 languages; no secrets in screen share. Free roam from specific chapter and return to sequence. Option to share QR/link so viewer opens same safe scene on own phone. Never auto-deploy, record, track viewers or claim connected CRM. Preload lightweight key scenes on Wi-Fi with budget and consent, not giant assets.

## White-Label Platform (P2 architecture now, dashboard later)
Separate reusable engine from fixture project identity. Single configuration schema for branding, project slug, multilingual strings, materials, floor mapping, facilities, camera anchors, optional featured tours, opt-in features, ownership footer and developer contacts. A new project must be loadable without rewriting core renderer logic and without inheriting Nakhla ownership claims. Validate each imported project's asset licensing, structural dimensions, fallback geometry, labels, UI localization keys and legal claims before publish. MVP: static tenant config + clean documentation; full version: permissioned CMS/editor/importer. Never host real client data or images in public repository without authorization. Do not transfer ownership of third-party assets by copying copyright footer.

## AI Property Concierge (P2 optional)
Start with offline FAQ from source-backed project facts and transparent limits, in a quiet 'Help' drawer. No intrusive floating AI avatar/chat bubble. Offers scripted common Q&A and links to floor/unit/material/amenity. If later adding LLM: user must approve API cost, privacy notices, server proxy, rate limit, content validation and evidence citations; refuse to invent prices, distances, performance claims, availability, feasibility, material certifications. Human contact CTA points to Rashid Omar Mohammed as demo creator, never to a fictional property seller. No unsolicited emails/messages.

## Personalization value and privacy
No forced authentication, cookies/analytics/tracking without approval; ideally first release operates locally with optional shared state URL. If an eventual CRM/booking flow is proposed, implement a truthful 'Request project demo' to creator only when explicitly approved to transmit visitor data; until then mailto/tel link or form with 'preview only' label. Respect source reel licenses and brand distinction.

specs/V5_PERFORMANCE_ACCESSIBILITY_AND_INTEGRATION.md

# V5 — INTEGRATION, RELIABILITY, INTERACTION AND PERFORMANCE HARD GATES

## Architecture: one scene, one truth, one navigation owner
- V3 `explorationMode` remains single source of camera controller ownership, NOT top-level Section. New `activeSection` UI state is 'explore','tour','design','amenities' only. `viewMode` is a visual composition descriptor, not another controller. `activeTool` is contextual overlay enum, not navigation mode. Never introduce a second global animation RAF or camera ownership state.
- Store core domain selection in one typed canonical state: selectedFloorId/unitId/roomId, apartmentCompareIds max length 2, gallery/preset, finishOverrides and history, camera bookmarks, environment, locale, sound, and new section/tool; preserve verified scene state across transitions.
- Device quality auto mode uses measured stable FPS, thermal/battery heuristics where supported, not guessed GPU capabilities. Lite fallback retains sections, plans, compare, material metadata, localized contact and exports. Optional weather/time/sun effects degrade before interaction, not after.
- Long-running renderer scenes and asset downloads must be cancellable. Lazy load section-specific assets. Keep one WebGL renderer; free memory after leaving amenity scenes where safe. No eager download of all apartments, 3D furniture and cinematic videos. Texture atlases/LOD/instancing/compressed glTF as appropriate after evidence, not blindly.
- Share/QR and PDF generation should not add heavy startup JS. Use dynamic imports and a 2D safe fallback. AI API is DISABLED BY DEFAULT.
- No P0 navigation dead ends, GPU crash blank page, stuck camera on mobile, unbounded audio, shader compilation freeze, focus trap or state-loss on mode/locale changes.

## User path and mental load
- First visit: optional hero; click 'Explore' within one screen; four section labels visible. Context menu not more than 3 meaningful quick choices at once; additional options in named collapsible sheets. Hide advanced tooling from first-time user until selected floor/room. Avoid multi-modal pop-ups on mobile.
- Maintain same position when section changes, except intentional room transition; show breadcrumb and one-tap Return/Reset Camera. Overlays have dedicated X, keyboard Esc, swipe close mobile, preserved orientation when closing. Avoid overwriting user-chosen camera when someone starts guided tour after exploring; explicit prompts only.
- Avoid spinner storms with image skeleton/loading steps and meaningful fallback; do not make user wait for full scene to read specs or change languages.
- Do not autoplay audio or camera tour; audio always OFF on hard page load, even if previously enabled. Restore audio from opt-in only within same page session, never from persisted state. Demo environment simulated by default and labelled accordingly.

## Visual integrity
- Four locales complete, context-aware copy and proofreading sign-offs; aria names and generated exports included. Arabic and Sorani RTL, English and Simplified Chinese LTR; do not flip real-world compass and scene normals with RTL. Mixed email/phone remain readable and callable.
- Keep warm limestone/bronze understated design, clear hierarchy, consistent spacing grid, text tokens responsive, tactile hover/focus/pressed, high contrast and minimum 44px target. Finite micro-animations: typically 180–350ms with easy ease-out and reduced motion alternative. No scrolljacking, heavy backdrop filter layers, particle storms or aggressive camera accelerations.
- Personal data never in share token by default; creator contact on footer, About and project PDF only (explicitly provided), not as an invented real-estate agency. No silent transmission from visitors.

## Measurable technical gate
Reapply original performance matrix V2 + E2E tests after V5. Profile mid-range Android, recent iOS, iPadOS/tablet, modest Windows PC, and no-WebGL Lite as available with browser/device evidence. Check mobile 320/360/390/768px, desktop 1024/1440px, landscape, touch keyboard, 200% zoom, low-end GPU, data saver, simulated slow 4G, repeated mode/locale/weather changes and WebGL context loss. Track JS payload, exterior/interior GLB size, LCP, INP, CLS, median + p95 FPS when rotating/walking, memory trend for repeated tours, browser console errors, degraded state, screenshot and video snapshots. Use baseline and measure before/after the V5 features. If an enhancement pushes measured P0 limits or lacks correct fallback, turn OFF by default and log reason rather than shipping lag.

## Implementation dependency order
P0: four sections + original V4 baseline, navigation state & accessibility. P1: finder + compare + share, illustrative sun/view, day-in-life, sales presentation. P2: furniture measurements, X-Ray Pro, AI style suggestions, FAQ/concierge, configurable white label architecture. These are not additional visible primary sections. Each component has a real test and release gate before enabling. User is authorized to choose aesthetic/implementation details and free vetted skills, but not paid tools, public deployments or contacting companies.

qa/V5_RELEASE_GATES.md

# V5 — RELEASE GATES: PROVE THE SIMPLE FOUR-SECTION EXPERIENCE WORKS

Fixture validation is not product testing; start all new cases NOT_RUN, only PASS with actual evidence path, tested device/browser/build. P0 failure blocks claiming product ready.

## P0 blocking UX / data / safety
1. Exactly four clear top-level visitor sections in all 4 locales; optional landing is a hero not fifth permanent tab; activeTool and camera mode do not become navigation tabs.
2. Arabic is first-visit default; all UI copy including contextual tools, share, FAQ, presentations and exports fully localized and reviewed status recorded, RTL/CKB and CJK glyphs correct.
3. Audio OFF on new page load no autoplay; no tour/floor/section/language/weather change unmutes. Sound control always works with keyboard/touch.
4. No blank 3D screen as only way to access information; Lite can browse floors/unit list/materials/gallery/summary/amenities/contact and compare.
5. 12 levels G+01..11 with exactly 18 units on F01–F09 only; no units on F10/F11. Creator phone and email exact, no fictional sales ownership.
6. All four exploration camera modes share one controller owner; no camera clipping, stuck pointer-lock, rollercoaster animation, surprise teleports or overlap of inconsistent scene positions.
7. Multi-section flow preserves selected unit, material changes, bookmarked camera, weather and language; navigating to amenities does not reassign the unit onto F10/F11.
8. Link share is bounded/allowlisted/no personally identifying visitor data; failure falls back to copyable summary; shared state tamper/invalid IDs safely handled.
9. All conceptual views, daylight, size and furnishings marked illustrative unless based on approved survey/drawings; no fake prices or availability.
10. No unreviewed external skills, paid services, auto-contact or public deployment.

## P1 value/experience QA
- Smart Apartment Finder: deterministic result/why/explain/reset/empty state, respects floor/type catalog; no sensitive prompts; scores not fabricated.
- Compare two apartments only, not accidental 3; correct actual differences and return to previous selected unit.
- Daylight and View Lens camera/time changes cause observable result without scene flicker; unverifiable views clearly conceptual; north position consistent across languages.
- Day-in-life chapter skip/pause, environment restore, reduced-motion+Lite chapter cards, no giant start download or audio startup.
- Presentation action is nonintrusive; salesperson can jump to selected floor/room and return without data loss; 3–5min chapter script (not forced playback).
- Save choices survives section switches; print/export/share works in all locales with caveats and correct materials; no network calls required.

## P2 conditional QA
- Furniture models stay inside room, measured dimensions anchored to actual GLB geometry; incorrect source data forces 'illustrative' mode; touch alternative.
- X-Ray uses real meshes; no fabricated pipes and no material corruption when exiting, graceful mobile fallback.
- Style suggestions only use catalog IDs and are reversible; optional AI API disabled, never demands an API key to open site.
- FAQ concierge cites available project metadata, returns 'unknown' when not sourced; no hallucinatory pricing or private-data collection.
- White-label project schema can switch to second clearly fictional seeded project with no Nakhla-specific old data or creator leakage; no developer-only keys in public runtime.

## Human usability challenge
Observe one uninstructed new visitor on phone: reaches Floor 04 Unit A, finds Living, changes floor material, switches to Amenities, returns and sends a copied selection within a few minutes. Record time, mistakes, missed controls and confusion; refine until no critical stalls. Testing without real users may be simulated but must NOT be misreported as a real usability study.

## Tradeoff rule
Track performance and regressions BEFORE enabling each P1/P2 feature. A genuinely polished P0/P1 with P2 documented as roadmap is better than a bloated unresponsive interface. Mark every item SHIPPED/FEATURE_FLAGGED/ROADMAP/BLOCKED with evidence, never all PASS by default.

### specs/V6_AI_PROPERTY_EXPERT.md
=================================================================
# V6 — NAKHLA AI: PRODUCT REQUIREMENTS / COMPLETE QUESTION-ANSWER COVERAGE

## 0. Nonnegotiable definition of done
Ship a demonstrably WORKING, contextual, multilingual question assistant that gets its answers from canonical, versioned property records. It MUST work in the zero-budget demo without any external API, cloud key, signup, account, microphone, heavy ML model or separate server. All answers must be testable. It must never invent missing dimensions, price, warranty, construction certification, supplier, availability, pool depth or operating hours. Be useful on the first visit and invisible when not wanted. Generative LLM sophistication is optional, not a release dependency. If a local intent engine is used, label it responsibly, rather than falsely suggesting paid generative intelligence.

## 1. Its job is to understand the exact current context
Read from the application's single source of truth: projectId, activeSection, activeFloorId, activeUnitId, activeRoomId, hovered/selectedSurfaceId, material overrides, finish package, environment and current locale. On a user utterance, snapshot context at query time, assign a queryId; if context changes before finishing, cancel or mark stale and never use old selection to navigate. Use explicit entity names in user's message ahead of default context. "This floor", "هاي الشقة", "هنا", "هاي الأرضية" should resolve to current selection only if unambiguous; otherwise ask ONE friendly follow-up: "تقصد أرضية الصالة لو جدار المطبخ؟". After a material change, current material answer must reflect the new material ID. After changing from Type A to B, clear invalid room IDs and do not keep Type A answers.

## 2. Detailed knowledge domains: every area needs structured, accurate answers
A. Building/tower: fictional demo location, 12 total occupied floors = Ground + F01–F09 residential + F10 gym/pool/spa + F11 restaurant/café/separate conditional shisha/terrace; 18 fictional units total. Explain concept status and safety caveats where relevant.
B. Floor: index, purpose, units on residential levels, number of bedrooms per unit type, floor level and amenities, how to jump there. Never claim sale availability.
C. Unit: ID, selected floor, type A/B, bedrooms/bathrooms, DEMO approximate 125/145 m², area basis unknown, rooms available, balcony, finish plan; compare two actual selected IDs with equivalent area definitions. Never claim 125 is net usable measured area.
D. Rooms: room labels, IDs, shape/geometry only once authored, dimensions length/width/height (with units + method + last validated version), area from floor polygon excluding holes (m²), ceiling height, doors/windows/openings, room adjacency, accessible route and camera waypoint. Null dimensions MUST produce the honest unknown answer. If polygon is irregular, do not claim one L×W pair; show bounded dimensions and measured area separately.
E. Measurement objects: selected wall length, window width/height, door clearance, balcony dimensions, furniture dimensions/fit, floor-to-ceiling, distances, area and optional volume. Coordinates from THREE world transforms must be converted to meters with a declared model scale/axis. Use geometric measurements only if scene geometry and CAD model reconcile and are inspected. Report precision consistent with data; label visual estimates as approximate and not construction measurements.
F. Materials: identify the exact selected surface and material currently assigned, category (floor/wall/wood/cabinet/counter/bathroom), descriptive appearance, supplier/manufacturer/origin only when confirmed, size, finish matte/gloss, slip resistance/wet-area suitability ONLY if sourced, maintenance guidance only if source-approved, material codes, comparison of active alternatives, per-room default and changed options. Demonstration color swatches are not technical specifications; do not confuse marble-look porcelain with natural marble. A material passport should report missing fields as 'not supplied', not omit them to imply certification.
G. Weather/sun/view: how to change hour, season, clouds/weather, daytime shadows, orientation if geometry metadata verified, balcony view as conceptual if not surveyed. Do not make precise hours of sunlight exposure based on a marketing render; if simulation isn't georeferenced, say illustrative.
H. Amenities: where gym, pool, spa and restaurant/café/terrace are; routes from selected unit and contextually jump; operating hours, access restrictions, prices, availability, pool capacity/depth and food menu = UNKNOWN without approved data. Shisha must always have age/legal/smoke safety caveat without presenting it as a confirmed operating service.
I. Packages and compare: explain Standard/Premium/Luxury equivalent conceptual presets as defined in `finish_packages.seed.json`, show which exact catalog IDs are applied, differences, and what is customizable; show before/after with user confirmation. NEVER fabricate upgrade pricing or guarantees.
J. Unit and floor discovery: "Which is largest?", "أريد شقة بثلاث غرف", "أي طابق بيه كوفي؟", "شلون أطلع للطابق 7؟", "قارن 04A و07B"; calculate from canonical catalog, with honest explanation. No hallucinated commercial inventory.
K. Sales and ownership: demo's creator is Rashid Omar Mohammed, +9647700864990, rashid.iq12@gmail.com. These contacts refer to creator / demo service not an actual Nakhla development sales office. Provide public click-to-contact links only on explicit user interaction. Do not auto-send, autofill personal details or expose visitor data.
L. Navigation/how-to: how to use Orbit, Property Explorer, Guided, Cinematic, Free Walk, Lite, mobile controls, sound control, time/season settings, back, reset view, screenshot and save; answers depend on features truly implemented and available.
M. Architectural engineering: explain conceptual wall/insulation/shading/sustainability claims only where evidence supports; do not assert structural feasibility, fire compliance, HVAC design, pool loading approval, exact performance, or building permit.
N. Financing/property: mortgage, investment yields, legal status, availability, rental income and sold status require actual verified source and relevant disclaimers. In a fictional demo respond 'not available as this is a concept', never invent calculations.
O. Accessibility: navigable route/step-free access/elevator dimensions only when verified; do not promise full accessibility of unbuilt project.
P. Documents: show material spec sheet, PDF, unit plan, project brochure if actual file exists. No fake download buttons or generated false certification.

## 3. Concrete question/answer acceptance examples (Iraqi Arabic)
- User at F04 Unit U04A: "شكد مساحة الشقة؟" -> "الشقة 04A نوع A، مساحتها التصورية حوالي 125 م². هذه مساحة افتراضية لنموذج العرض وليست قياساً معتمداً." + action "عرض المخطط".
- User in living room: "شكد طول الصالة؟" -> if `rooms[living].dimensions_m` null: "طول الصالة مو مثبت بعد ضمن المخطط التجريبي؛ ما أريد أعطيك رقم غير دقيق. أكدر أعرضلك المخطط لما تتوثق الأبعاد." Not invent "6 × 4".
- User clicked kitchen cabinetry: "شنو نوع هذا الخشب؟" -> access CURRENT material ID and exact record; if concept walnut-finish cabinet reply clearly finish sample + no verified manufacturer, NOT species proof. Show Material Passport.
- User: "أي طابق بيه المسبح؟" -> F10 / tenth floor. Action "ودّيني للمسبح" is typed `navigateToAmenity` not string-executed code.
- User: "أريد أكبر شقة" -> Type B 145m² approx vs Type A 125m² approx, with demo caveat; may show Type B entries.
- User: "هذا البلاط يتحمل الماي؟" -> if no certified slip/wet rating: "ما عدنا تصنيف مقاومة الانزلاق أو ملاءمة المناطق الرطبة مؤكد لهذه العينة. لازم نرجع لورقة المورد." Never guess based on product appearance.
- User: "انطيني السعر" -> "المشروع نموذج عرض، وماكو سعر بيع معتمد متوفر." Never imply listed for sale.
- User: "شنو الفرق بين 03A و05B؟" -> Type A vs Type B, current floor IDs, bedrooms, demo approximate areas; compare type templates and disclose same demo repetition.
- User: "بدل الأرضية رخام" -> show eligible materials, preview, request explicit confirmation before commit, and avoid wrongly describing marble-look porcelain as natural marble.
- User: "أخذني لغرفة النوم الرئيسية" -> only when selected unit has verified navigation waypoint; otherwise show room list / 2D Lite fallback.
- User: "كم سعر الإيجار وشنو العمولة؟" -> unavailable for fictional project, may refer to demo creator only for product-service questions, not home sales.
- User: "هل الموقع يدعم الكردية؟" -> yes if tested UI locale available; if native linguistic review incomplete, don't claim fully proofread.
- User: "منو مسوي هذا الموقع؟" -> creator name and accurate provided public contact; avoid saying owner of building.
- User: "عدد طوابق العمارة 10 لو 12؟" -> exactly 12 including ground, 9 residential + 2 amenity, explain counts and avoid the old 10-level draft.
- User: "شنو ارتفاع السقف؟" -> if only floor-to-floor exists, say floor-to-floor is NOT finished clear ceiling height; do not confuse.

## 4. Visual interaction, not chat wall
A small "اسأل نخلة" help button (not fifth top-level tab). Initial quick chips vary by location: outside tower -> "كم طابق؟", "وين المسبح؟", "دلني على شقة"; within selected unit -> "شكد المساحة؟", "قارن شقق", "اختيار المواد"; in a room -> "شنو القياسات؟", "نوع الأرضية؟", "وشنو أگدر أغير؟"; selected surface -> "شنو هذي المادة؟", "عرض المواصفات", "بدائل"; selected amenity -> "وين يقع؟", "شنو بيه؟". Chips are suggestions; free-text question works as well and has a graceful unknown path. No intrusive auto-open/toast. The assistant should offer at most 2 optional next actions.

## 5. Trust labels
- VERIFIED_SOURCE: verified sourced dimension/supplier/certification, exact fact, record URI/version/date, only after authentic approval.
- CONCEPT_ESTIMATE: architecture design model, approximated area, illustrative sun/view/measurements; must say "تصوري/افتراضي" in user-visible answer even if user did not ask.
- FACT_FROM_DEMO_SCHEMA: topology and room type in fiction; factual about this specific invented demo but NOT actual permitted property.
- UNKNOWN_OR_NOT_PROVIDED: null fields; answer honestly with likely next step.
- PROPOSAL_OR_SUGGESTION: preference recommendations, not facts or promises.
Never use an unqualified "100%" confidence or invented external citation. On action answer, always keep label/data provenance accessible to the user. After a data update and material switch answers must update without stale cache.

## 6. Upgrade roadmap
P0 = deterministic multi-domain source-grounded property answer + source/caveats + context + selected entity + helpful links/actions + four-language UI and mobile. P1 = ask-about-selected 3D object, explain-compare, richer fuzzy Arabic/English/CKB/Chinese variations, source trace, design suggestions as rules, optional push-to-talk WITHOUT speech unless independent explicit consent and safe design. P2 = verified backend LLM/RAG or on-prem small model, only if measurable uplift, feasible inference, cost/privacy approval and infrastructure. Don't add AI complexity that harms main 3D experience.

=================================================================
### specs/V6_AI_GROUNDING_ENGINE_AND_ACTIONS.md
=================================================================
# V6 — ENGINEERING CONTRACT FOR GROUNDED CONTEXTUAL QUESTIONS, ACTIONS AND ZERO-HALLUCINATION DATA

## The only correct source of answers
Build one typed `PropertyKnowledgeGraph` as a SELECTOR/ADAPTER of existing tower_concept, navigation_manifest, room_program, material_catalog, finish_packages, geometry metadata and actual live state; do not manually replicate data in code or prompt. The demo source files are not construction-approved. Treat every field's provenance and verification status as data. Deployers can later switch to approved client datasets through white-label schema without changing behavior.

Mandatory entity IDs: projectId, floorId, unitId, unitTypeId, roomId, selectedSurfaceId, materialId, finishPackageId, amenityId. Canonical ownership of selection remains core app state. `sourceRecordRef`, `sourceHash`, `geometryRevision`, `verificationStatus`, `verifiedBy`, `verifiedAt`, `areaBasis`, `unit`, `precision`, `lastUpdated` are supported fields, never fake-filled. If display label changed language, ID must not change. `room dimensions = null` is a meaningful fact; never fill with derived guessed rectangles. If CAD DXF dimensions are inconsistent with Blender mesh scale, mark invalid/UNKNOWN and block answering dimension values until resolved.

## Answer data flow
User text -> locale-normalized (NFKC where suitable, preserve Kurdish/Chinese semantics) -> PII-safe minimal query -> IntentClassifier/fuzzy lexical index -> EntityResolver (explicit IDs first, then selected unit/mesh/room, never hidden guess) -> DomainSelector -> FactCalculator/GeometryVerifier -> AnswerComposer(locale) -> ResponseEnvelope + suggested validated UI action -> accessible in-context display. A low-cost client-only engine suffices for P0. Do not send input to any third party by default. If no confident intent -> 3 relevant query chips and concise explanation, not fabricated answer.

TypeScript type expectations, implement production code not this text as-is:
```ts
type FactStatus = 'verified_source' | 'concept_estimate' | 'demo_schema' | 'unknown' | 'proposal';
type AnswerEnvelope = {
  questionId: string;
  text: string;
  locale: 'ar'|'en'|'ckb'|'zh';
  status: FactStatus;
  citations: Array<{sourceId: string; fieldPath: string; revision?: string; label?: string}>;
  entityContext: {floorId?: string; unitId?: string; roomId?: string; surfaceId?: string; materialId?: string};
  suggestions: Array<{labelKey: string; toolActionId: string; args: Record<string,string>}>;
  uncertainty?: string;
  answeredAt: string;
};
```
Citations are INTERNAL property-source pointers, not fake website citations. The UI can show source-label or disclaimer. If a fact has `status: unknown`, ensure no numeric value is generated.

## Geometry measurement rules (P0 source validation, P1 interactive measurement)
1. Define a project-wide meters scale/axis, glTF coordinate orientation and transform version. NEVER assume raw scene unit = meter without verifying.
2. Differentiate nominal room bounding box, true usable room polygon, openable volume, floor-to-floor and finished ceiling height; report exact basis and units.
3. Calculate room footprint polygon area with holes excluded; geometry can be nonrectangular, don't output fabricated L×W. A room-specific numeric answer requires audited polygon or dimension record, not merely approximate building footprint.
4. Wall length from actual selected wall mesh, endpoints/transforms; no magical raycast pixel estimate. Door/window clear openings from opening schedule. Pool dimensions, depth, width, floor slab loads require verified architecture and structural records.
5. Precision follows input. Prefer 1 decimal meter where physically warranted, no "1.234567m" from imperfect mesh. For concept data clearly identify as illustrative.
6. On suspicious mismatch (computed area > containing unit area; window > wall; invalid polygons; mismatched vertical heights; floor wrong; room intersects corridor), fail safe to unavailable and open internal validation issue.
7. If future real approved CAD/BIM import, record author/approval, immutable revision, measurement method, units, floor mapping, privacy/usage permissions before verified labels.

## Material state
selectedSurfaceId -> exactly one semantic material slot -> current active finishPackage/base materialId -> applied user override if present -> catalog record. Never answer on base package if current override changed. Distinguish appearance / grade / brand / origin / dimensions / price / wet-rating / slip class, and leave absent fields UNKNOWN. If category invalid for selected surface, do not apply replacement; show applicable alternatives. Re-render material first after explicit user confirmation; assistant replies using committed active ID; if user cancels restore previous choice. Material passport and assistant must agree with 3D shader and PDF.

## Typed actions, never free text execution
Implement a fixed registry; each action validates arguments against canonical entity ID allowlists, active capability, route, state transition and permission before firing. Supported examples: `selectFloor`, `selectUnit`, `focusRoom`, `focusSurface`, `openMaterialPassport`, `openFloorPlan`, `openComparison`, `openAmenities`, `navigateToAmenity`, `openMeasurements`, `openFinishEditor`, `previewMaterial`, `confirmMaterialChange`, `resetCamera`, `show2DAlternative`, `openCreatorContact`, `openPrintSummary`. NEVER allow AI-generated JavaScript, arbitrary routes, external URLs, `eval`, OS commands, API tokens, background email/calls, purchasing, booking or changing pricing/availability.
- Text-only factual answers need no UI state change.
- "خذني للمسبح" -> show action button and upon click move to F10/indoor_pool; if user's navigation framework permits immediate intent navigation, user message itself is the explicit action, but still validate IDs, preserve prior selection and offer Back.
- "بدل المادة" -> show allowed preview options; confirmation for committing customization (undo available).
- "أرسل مواصفاتي" -> show copy/share preview and explicit separate submit, no automatic messaging.
- If 3D mesh not loaded / low device performance -> same information + 2D navigable UI.

## Generative extensions OPTIONAL and gated
Do not invent a local unrestricted ChatGPT if no model present. An optional LLM (API or locally run) MAY be used only if enabled by owner following cost/privacy/security evaluation; never embed provider keys in shipped JS or static metadata. Must use retrieval and structured tools; deny hallucinated direct free-form facts. Strict schema-validate tools. System-level LLM instructions independent from user inputs; retrieve text as untrusted data. Minimize and redact visitor PII, no stored raw chats by default, privacy notice, limited retention with opt-in. Rate limit, timeout, safe fallbacks to local deterministic engine. Third-party model access not allowed before explicit approval. No hidden billing or required user-provided credentials. All facts from canonical app metadata and citations are revalidated by server before response.

## Code architecture and ownership
Suggested logical modules: `assistant/useContextSnapshot.ts`, `assistant/intentIndex.ts`, `assistant/factResolver.ts`, `assistant/geometryAdapter.ts`, `assistant/materialResolver.ts`, `assistant/answerTemplates.ts`, `assistant/actionRegistry.ts`, `assistant/assistantStore.ts`, `assistant/i18n.ts`, `assistant/AssistantPanel.tsx`, `assistant/assistant.spec.ts`; actual framework design decision to be researched. Prefer code splitting / dynamic import. No second WebGL renderer, no AI polling, no hidden GPU work while assistant closed. Memory bounded to current session; locale/context changes invalidate stale reply. Use React Suspense/progressive enhancement as appropriate, no UI thread stalls.

### specs/V6_AI_CONTEXTUAL_UI_I18N_PRIVACY.md
=================================================================
# V6 — CONTEXTUAL AI PRODUCT DESIGN: CONVERSATIONAL SIMPLICITY, FOUR LOCALES, PRIVACY AND PERFORMANCE

## UX principle
The main nav stays Explore/Tour/Design/Amenities ONLY, in four languages. Place a small accessible "اسأل نخلة" button in existing Help utility / contextual bubble. Not a fifth navigation item. On desktop open compact bottom-right side sheet (avoid 3D center); on mobile and iPad use a dismissible bottom sheet with safe-area insets, avoid obscuring essential bottom navigation, a draggable/collapsible header and max height that keeps the model visible; keyboard remains usable, Escape/back closes, focus managed, no modal trapping unnecessarily. When minimized, assistant consumes almost no screen. No conversation popups automatically on page load or camera transitions. No "AI assistant is typing" animation unless real; no faked human typing, forced tours, banners, confetti or marketing spam.

## Question entry and suggestions
Localized small placeholder, submit icon, accessible keyboard shortcuts, explicit send, loading/cancel, retry, helpful unknown, visible source status. Suggested quick questions vary by building/floor/unit/room/material/amenity context but never more than 3–4 chips simultaneously. Chips should not exceed a line in mobile or use accessible horizontally scrolling container with logical focus order. E.g. 'شكد مساحة الشقة؟', 'شنو المواد؟', 'وين المسبح؟' outside; 'كم طول هذا الجدار؟', 'نوع الخشب؟' when selected. Tapping a 3D object can offer a small unobtrusive 'اسأل عن هذا' card with exact semantic object ID attached, not arbitrary image guess. All controls work in Lite mode with semantic list selection.

## Conversation behavior
Concise direct answer first; supporting detail only on expansion. Cite precise fact origin and demo-status badge, e.g. 'المصدر: مخطط النموذج التجريبي — غير معتمد'. Show up to two meaningful actions: 'عرض المخطط', 'الانتقال للطابق', 'شوف البدائل'. User chooses to navigate; do not teleport without intent. Previous question memory for anaphora ('زين شكد عرضها؟') scoped to currently selected object; invalidate when scene context changes and offer a clarification where ambiguous. Provide clear way to restart chat/clear conversation; default no cloud history.

## Four languages
Arabic ar-IQ DEFAULT; English en; Kurdish Sorani ckb-IQ RTL; Chinese Simplified zh-Hans. The assistant must support each in request entry, intent variations, response templates, source status labels, quick questions, errors, validations, names, units, number formatting, chips, share previews, generated reports, and accessibility labels. Actual source facts remain stable IDs, translated display only. English, Sorani and Chinese translation dictionaries cannot be declared native-proofread by code. Require competent human review before production publication; label draft content accordingly. Ensure Arabic "طول" vs "عرض" vs "مساحة" don't collapse, Chinese long/width/area terminology and Sorani script direction are correct in final QA. Expose optional robust unit conversion and localized numerals without confusing mm, m and m². Use BCP47/i18n ICU formatting and correct bidirectional handling for `F04`, `U04A`, IDs, decimals, phone and email in RTL text. No auto-language switch based on guessed ethnicity or geolocation; obey chosen UI language. In mixed-language questions, support known material English terms as synonyms.

## Audio / voice
Sound always disabled on EACH full page load. AI answers are text only by default. No voice synthesis, no microphone, no ambient audio or speech recognition auto-start; 'Start Tour' does not unmute. If future user opts into audio, provide separate disclosure for microphone/STT and TTS, stop, mute, language-specific consent, no automatic network streaming. Accessibility should not require audio.

## Privacy and security
Zero-login default, no analytics of chat text/selected unit/phone without explicit policy and permission. Keep chat transient in tab memory or local ephemeral state; clear on reload by default; 'Save My Design' share schema must never include messages, names, email, phone or untrusted prompt content. Never treat user chat as instructions to modify site source, buy items, email the creator, or unlock hidden tools. Limit message size, escape render (no arbitrary HTML), sanitize Markdown, disallow markdown image/JS URI injection, validate ID-based actions. On failure provide local answer fallback and offline FAQ, no silent personal data uploads. Public contact details of demo creator can be shown; never append them automatically to sensitive third-party AI prompts.

## Performance and usability acceptance
- No model weights (100 MB+) bundled into browser; no large NLP runtime at startup. Assistant JS loaded only on first open or when safe; pre-index cheap metadata without blocking first interactive view.
- Minimal CPU memory/GC; virtualized/bounded message history; constant-time known-entity fact lookup where possible. No secondary WebGL canvas. React rendering does not re-create geometries on every keystroke; maintain 3D FPS budget and deferred text matching.
- Text input remains responsive during camera changes; keyboard open/close doesn't resize canvas into a broken viewport. Use stable layout with visual viewport changes on iOS Safari/Android Chrome; test actual devices where possible.
- Results under 500 ms for simple local known questions on ordinary mid-tier desktop; define device-specific realistic budget via profiling rather than universal guaranteed latency. Never block the main UI if request takes longer; cancel/retry and fallback. Degrade suggestions gracefully on low-end.
- Works without WebGL and without network once app metadata cached; service worker offline mode only if properly designed, optional.
- Keyboard screen-reader semantics: role/aria for answer, transcript, source labels and actions, proper focus, visible outline, large touch hit zones (~44 CSS px), respects prefers-reduced-motion and viewport/safe areas. WCAG contrast targets consistent with V3 UX contract.

## Creative features that are genuinely helpful
- 'Point and Ask': tap wall/window/floor/cabinet and ask facts from source mesh/material, not vision guess.
- 'Explain the Difference': side-by-side factual answer for two floorplans or two finish packages; user can jump to compare view.
- 'Show Me': assistant answer can turn into validated camera highlight or contextual help indicator; no surprise teleport while user is touring.
- 'Where Am I?': short breadcrumb+unit/room, option to go home, never geo-location tracking.
- 'Design Reasoning': transparent rules why finish sample fits palette; clearly recommendation not architectural proof.
- 'Find My Room': uses navigable semantic graph / Lite route; stairs/elevator paths not fabricated if unbuilt.
- 'Quick Fact Lens': single tap shows an instant spec card without opening chat; assistant expands on request.

All features must be implemented based on actual capabilities with working controls; unimplemented advanced behavior is documented as roadmap, not misleading clickable placeholders.

### research/V6_AI_SKILLS_AND_COST_PROTOCOL.md
=================================================================
# V6 — AI RESEARCH, RELEVANT SKILLS AND COST APPROVAL PROTOCOL
Research available project-scoped tools rather than blindly installing popular AI agents. Claude may inspect environment, find and vet reliable open-source libraries for localized intent detection / typo handling / transliteration, React accessibility, schema validation (Zod/JSON Schema), geometry measurement, state/store selectors, lightweight text search, safe PDF rendering, i18n and Playwright/browser QA. Verify source repository, license for commercial website, maintainer activity, security notices, transitive deps, package versions, file integrity and export size. Pin versions and install only after source review. Avoid overlapping libraries and huge SDKs. Use available already-installed skills before adding more; log installed tools and rationale in templates/SKILL_AUDIT_TEMPLATE.md.

Zero incremental budget is default: do NOT purchase inference APIs, cloud speech-to-text, GPU instances, LLM credits, translation subscriptions or SaaS without owner permission. Do not auto-enable external cloud calls to a stranger's LLM endpoint. The factual local assistant is the P0 baseline, preferably pure TypeScript and existing app data. Suggest an LLM option as a separate documented P2 tier if benefits vs costs/security justify it. If a model could be run server-side for a future commercial site, quantify actual infrastructure, latency, translations, data policy and abuse budget before recommending. Public access requires separate user authorization and privacy/legal review. No false claim of "AI reads any question" when local parser coverage is bounded; honest unknown answers are part of quality.
